    globalTeachingPlans=res.documents||[];populateTeachingPlanSubjectDropdown();renderTeachingPlanTable();
    var status=document.getElementById('teachingPlanUploadStatus');if(status)status.textContent='มีเอกสาร '+globalTeachingPlans.length+' ไฟล์';
  }).withFailureHandler(function(e){if(body)body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" class="p-5 text-center text-red-500">'+esc(e.message||e)+'</td></tr>';}).apiGetTeachingPlans(currentUser);
}
function renderTeachingPlanTable(){
  var body=document.getElementById('teachingPlanTableBody');if(!body)return;
  if(!globalTeachingPlans.length){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" class="p-5 text-center text-slate-400">ยังไม่มีเอกสารโครงการสอน กรุณาอัปโหลดไฟล์ .doc, .docx หรือ .pdf</td></tr>';return;}
  body.innerHTML=globalTeachingPlans.map(function(d){var subject=d.subjectCode?(d.subjectCode+' - '+d.subjectName):'ใช้ได้ทุกวิชา';var status=String(d.conversionStatus||'');var statusClass=(status.indexOf('ไม่สำเร็จ')>=0||status.indexOf('ไม่พบข้อความ')>=0)?'text-amber-700':status.indexOf('สำเร็จ')>=0?'text-emerald-700':'text-slate-600';return '<tr class="align-top"><td class="p-2 border font-semibold">'+esc(d.fileName)+'<div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-normal">.'+esc(d.extension)+'</div></td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(subject)+'</td><td class="p-2 border whitespace-nowrap">'+esc(d.uploadedAt)+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+(d.unitTitles||[]).length+'</td><td class="p-2 border '+statusClass+'">'+esc(status)+'</td><td class="p-2 border whitespace-nowrap"><button type="button" onclick="previewTeachingPlan(\\''+esc(d.documentId)+'\\')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 rounded">ดูข้อมูล</button> '+(d.fileUrl?'<a class="inline-block px-2 py-1 bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 rounded" href="'+esc(d.fileUrl)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">ไฟล์ต้นฉบับ</a>':'')+' <button type="button" onclick="deleteTeachingPlan(\\''+esc(d.documentId)+'\\')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-red-100 text-red-700 rounded">ลบ</button></td></tr>';}).join('');
}
function previewTeachingPlan(documentId){
  var d=globalTeachingPlans.find(function(x){return x.documentId===documentId;});if(!d)return;
  document.getElementById('teachingPlanPreviewTitle').textContent=d.fileName||'โครงการสอน';
  document.getElementById('teachingPlanPreviewMeta').textContent=(d.subjectCode?(d.subjectCode+' - '+d.subjectName+' · '):'')+'อัปโหลด '+(d.uploadedAt||'-')+' · '+((d.unitTitles||[]).length)+' ชื่อเรื่อง · '+(d.conversionStatus||'');
  var original=document.getElementById('teachingPlanOriginalLink');original.href=d.fileUrl||'#';original.classList.toggle('hidden',!d.fileUrl);
  var converted=document.getElementById('teachingPlanConvertedLink');converted.href=d.convertedUrl||'#';converted.classList.toggle('hidden',!d.convertedUrl);
  var titles=document.getElementById('teachingPlanUnitTitlesPreview');
  titles.innerHTML=(d.unitTitles||[]).length?(d.unitTitles||[]).map(function(title){return '<span class="inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-3 py-1 bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 text-xs">'+esc(title)+'</span>';}).join(''):'<span class="text-xs text-slate-500">ไม่พบชื่อเรื่องอัตโนมัติ สามารถเปิดเอกสารต้นฉบับเพื่อตรวจสอบ และพิมพ์ชื่อกิจกรรมเองได้</span>';
  document.getElementById('teachingPlanExtractedText').textContent=d.extractedText||'ยังไม่มีข้อความที่แปลงได้จากเอกสารนี้';
  document.getElementById('teachingPlanPreviewPanel').classList.remove('hidden');document.getElementById('teachingPlanPreviewPanel').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});
}
function deleteTeachingPlan(documentId){
  var d=globalTeachingPlans.find(function(x){return x.documentId===documentId;});
  Swal.fire({icon:'warning',title:'ยืนยันลบเอกสารโครงการสอน?',text:d?d.fileName:'เอกสารนี้',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'ลบเอกสาร',cancelButtonText:'ยกเลิก'}).then(function(result){if(!result.isConfirmed)return;google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(res&&res.success){document.getElementById('teachingPlanPreviewPanel').classList.add('hidden');Swal.fire('ลบสำเร็จ',res.message,'success');loadTeachingPlans();if(document.getElementById('activitySubjectSelect').value)loadActivityFormOptions();}else showError(res&&res.message||'ลบเอกสารไม่สำเร็จ');}).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);}).apiDeleteTeachingPlan(currentUser,documentId);});
}
document.getElementById('teachingPlanUploadForm').addEventListener('submit',function(e){
  e.preventDefault();var input=document.getElementById('teachingPlanFileInput'),file=input&&input.files&&input.files[0];if(!file){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกไฟล์โครงการสอน','','warning');return;}
  if(!/\.(docx?|pdf)$/i.test(file.name)){Swal.fire('ชนิดไฟล์ไม่รองรับ','กรุณาเลือกไฟล์ .doc, .docx หรือ .pdf','warning');return;}
  if(file.size>8*1024*1024){Swal.fire('ไฟล์มีขนาดใหญ่เกินไป','จำกัดขนาดไม่เกิน 8 MB','warning');return;}
  var status=document.getElementById('teachingPlanUploadStatus');if(status)status.textContent='กำลังอ่านไฟล์...';
  var reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=function(){if(status)status.textContent='อ่านไฟล์ไม่สำเร็จ';showError('อ่านไฟล์ไม่สำเร็จ กรุณาลองใหม่');};
  reader.onload=function(){var base64=String(reader.result||'').split(',')[1]||'';Swal.fire({title:'กำลังอัปโหลดและแปลงเอกสาร...',text:'ระบบกำลังอ่านชื่อเรื่องในคอลัมน์หน่วยการเรียนรู้',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){Swal.close();if(res&&res.success){input.value='';if(status)status.textContent=res.message||'อัปโหลดเรียบร้อย';Swal.fire('อัปโหลดเสร็จสิ้น',res.message||'อัปโหลดโครงการสอนแล้ว','success');loadTeachingPlans();if(document.getElementById('activitySubjectSelect').value)loadActivityFormOptions();}else{if(status)status.textContent=res&&res.message||'อัปโหลดไม่สำเร็จ';showError(res&&res.message||'อัปโหลดไม่สำเร็จ');}}
    ).withFailureHandler(function(err){Swal.close();if(status)status.textContent='อัปโหลดไม่สำเร็จ';showError(err.message||err);}).apiUploadTeachingPlan(currentUser,{fileName:file.name,mimeType:file.type,base64:base64,subjectCode:document.getElementById('teachingPlanSubjectSelect').value});
  };reader.readAsDataURL(file);
});
function populateActivityProjectTitleDropdown(){
  var el=document.getElementById('activityProjectTitleSelect');if(!el)return;var old=el.value,currentTitle=document.getElementById('activityTitleInput').value;
  el.innerHTML='<option value="">-- เลือกชื่อเรื่องจากเอกสารโครงการสอน (ถ้ามี) --</option>'+globalActivityProjectTitles.map(function(x,i){return '<option value="'+esc(x.title)+'" data-document="'+esc(x.documentUrl||'')+'">'+esc(x.title)+(x.fileName?' · '+esc(x.fileName):'')+'</option>';}).join('');
  if(globalActivityProjectTitles.some(function(x){return x.title===old;}))el.value=old;
  if(currentTitle&&globalActivityProjectTitles.some(function(x){return x.title===currentTitle;}))el.value=currentTitle;
}

  var select=document.getElementById('activityProjectTitleSelect');if(!select||!select.value)return;
  document.getElementById('activityTitleInput').value=select.value;
  document.getElementById('activityTitleInput').focus();
}




// Learning activity management / activities for students
function loadLearningActivities(){
  if(!currentUser||currentUser.role==='Student')return;
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
    if(!res||!res.success){document.getElementById('learningActivitiesTableBody').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="10" class="p-5 text-center text-red-500">'+esc(res&&res.message||'โหลดกิจกรรมไม่สำเร็จ')+'</td></tr>';return;}
    globalTeacherSubjects=res.subjects||globalTeacherSubjects;globalLearningActivities=res.activities||[];
    populateActivitySubjectDropdown();renderLearningActivitiesTable();
  }).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);}).apiGetLearningActivities(currentUser);
}
function populateActivitySubjectDropdown(){
  var el=document.getElementById('activitySubjectSelect');if(!el)return;
  var old=el.value;el.innerHTML=globalTeacherSubjects.map(function(sub){return '<option value="'+esc(sub.subjectCode)+'">'+esc(sub.subjectCode+' - '+sub.subjectName)+'</option>';}).join('')||'<option value="">-- กรุณาเพิ่มรายวิชาก่อน --</option>';
  if(globalTeacherSubjects.some(function(sub){return sub.subjectCode===old;}))el.value=old;
  if(el.value)loadActivityFormOptions();
}
function loadActivityFormOptions(){
  var code=document.getElementById('activitySubjectSelect').value;
  if(pendingActivityTarget&&pendingActivityTarget.subjectCode!==code){pendingActivityTarget=null;pendingActivityWeek='';}
  if(!code){globalActivityRoster=[];globalActivityWeeks=[];globalActivityQuestionsByWeek={};fillActivityTargetDropdowns();return;}
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
    if(!res||!res.success){showError(res&&res.message||'โหลดรายชื่อและข้อสอบสำหรับกิจกรรมไม่สำเร็จ');return;}
    if(document.getElementById('activitySubjectSelect').value!==code)return;
    globalActivityRoster=res.roster||[];globalActivityWeeks=res.weeks||[];globalActivityQuestionsByWeek=res.questionsByWeek||{};globalActivityProjectTitles=res.projectTitles||[];
    populateActivityProjectTitleDropdown();fillActivityTargetDropdowns();populateActivityWeekDropdown(pendingActivityWeek||undefined);pendingActivityWeek='';
    if(pendingActivityTarget&&pendingActivityTarget.subjectCode===code){document.getElementById('activityGradeSelect').value=pendingActivityTarget.targetGrade||'';document.getElementById('activityRoomSelect').value=pendingActivityTarget.targetRoom||'';document.getElementById('activityStudentSelect').value=pendingActivityTarget.targetStudentId||'';pendingActivityTarget=null;updateActivityTargetControls();} 
  }).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);}).apiGetActivityFormOptions(currentUser,code);
}
function fillActivityTargetDropdowns(){
  var grade=document.getElementById('activityGradeSelect'),room=document.getElementById('activityRoomSelect'),student=document.getElementById('activityStudentSelect');if(!grade||!room||!student)return;
  var oldGrade=grade.value,oldRoom=room.value,oldStudent=student.value;
  var grades=Array.from(new Set(globalActivityRoster.map(function(x){return x.grade;}).filter(Boolean))).sort(function(a,b){return a.localeCompare(b,'th');});
  var rooms=Array.from(new Set(globalActivityRoster.map(function(x){return x.room;}).filter(Boolean))).sort(function(a,b){return a.localeCompare(b,'th');});
  grade.innerHTML='<option value="">เลือกระดับชั้น</option>'+grades.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x)+'">'+esc(x)+'</option>';}).join('');
  room.innerHTML='<option value="">เลือกห้อง</option>'+rooms.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x)+'">'+esc(x)+'</option>';}).join('');
  student.innerHTML='<option value="">เลือกนักเรียน</option>'+globalActivityRoster.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.studentId)+'">'+esc(x.studentId+' - '+x.studentName+' ('+x.room+')')+'</option>';}).join('');
  if(grades.indexOf(oldGrade)>=0)grade.value=oldGrade;if(rooms.indexOf(oldRoom)>=0)room.value=oldRoom;if(globalActivityRoster.some(function(x){return x.studentId===oldStudent;}))student.value=oldStudent;
  updateActivityTargetControls();
}
function populateActivityWeekDropdown(selected){
  var el=document.getElementById('activityWeekSelect');if(!el)return;
  var next=selected!=null&&String(selected)!==''?String(selected):String(el.value||'');
  if(next)el.value=next;
  updateActivityQuizControls();
}
function formatActivityDateBEClient(value){
  var text=String(value==null?'':value).trim();
  if(!text)return '';
  var m=text.match(/^(\\d{4})-(\\d{1,2})-(\\d{1,2})$/);
  if(m)return String(m[3]).padStart(2,'0')+'/'+String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0')+'/'+String(Number(m[1])+543);
  m=text.match(/^(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{4})$/);
  if(m){var y=Number(m[3]);if(y<2400)y+=543;return String(Number(m[1])).padStart(2,'0')+'/'+String(Number(m[2])).padStart(2,'0')+'/'+String(y);}
  return text;
}
function thaiDateInputToISOClient(value){
  var text=String(value==null?'':value).trim();
  var m=text.match(/^(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{4})$/);
  if(!m)return '';
  var day=Number(m[1]),month=Number(m[2]),year=Number(m[3]);if(year>=2400)year-=543;
  var d=new Date(year,month-1,day);
  if(d.getFullYear()!==year||d.getMonth()!==month-1||d.getDate()!==day)return '';
  return String(year)+'-'+String(month).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(day).padStart(2,'0');
}
function updateActivityTargetControls(){
  var type=document.getElementById('activityTargetTypeSelect').value;
  document.getElementById('activityGradeWrap').classList.toggle('hidden',type!=='LEVEL');
  document.getElementById('activityRoomWrap').classList.toggle('hidden',type!=='ROOM');
  document.getElementById('activityStudentWrap').classList.toggle('hidden',type!=='STUDENT');
}
function updateActivityQuizControls(){
  var mode=document.getElementById('activityQuizModeSelect').value;
  document.getElementById('activityQuestionNosWrap').classList.toggle('hidden',mode!=='SPECIFIC');
  document.getElementById('activityRandomCountWrap').classList.toggle('hidden',mode!=='RANDOM');
  var week=document.getElementById('activityWeekSelect');
  var summary=document.getElementById('activityWeekSummary');
  if(summary)summary.textContent=week&&week.value?'สัปดาห์ที่ '+week.value:'กรุณากำหนดสัปดาห์จากช่องด้านบน';
  document.getElementById('activityQuestionHelp').textContent=mode==='NONE'?'กิจกรรมนี้จะไม่กำหนดแบบทดสอบ แต่ยังต้องระบุสัปดาห์ของกิจกรรม':mode==='ALL'?'นักเรียนจะทำข้อสอบทุกข้อในสัปดาห์ที่กำหนดด้านบน':mode==='SPECIFIC'?'ระบุหมายเลขข้อที่ต้องการให้นักเรียนทำ ระบบจะตรวจว่าหมายเลขข้อมีอยู่จริงในสัปดาห์ที่กำหนด':'ระบบจะสุ่มข้อสอบตามจำนวนที่กำหนดจากสัปดาห์ที่กำหนด';
}
function formatActivityQuizLabel(a){if(a.quizMode==='NONE')return 'ไม่กำหนดข้อสอบ';if(a.quizMode==='ALL')return 'ทุกข้อ';if(a.quizMode==='SPECIFIC')return 'ข้อ '+(a.questionNos||[]).join(', ');return 'สุ่ม '+a.randomCount+' ข้อ';}
function selectedActivitySubmissionTypes(){
  if(document.getElementById('activityNoSubmission')&&document.getElementById('activityNoSubmission').checked)return [];
  return Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.activity-submission-type:checked')).map(function(el){return el.value;});
}
function updateActivitySubmissionControls(){
  var none=document.getElementById('activityNoSubmission'),wrap=document.getElementById('activitySubmissionTypesWrap'),help=document.getElementById('activitySubmissionHelp');
  var disabled=!!(none&&none.checked);
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.activity-submission-type'),function(el){el.disabled=disabled;});
  if(wrap)wrap.classList.toggle('opacity-40',disabled);
  if(help)help.textContent=disabled?'สัปดาห์นี้ระบบจะไม่แสดงช่องส่งชิ้นงานให้นักเรียน':'เลือกได้หลายรูปแบบ นักเรียนจะเห็นเฉพาะวิธีส่งที่เลือกไว้';
}
function activitySubmissionLabelClient(types){
  var labels={LINK:'ลิงก์เว็บไซต์',TEXT:'ข้อความ',IMAGE:'อัปโหลดรูปภาพ',CAMERA:'ถ่ายภาพจากกล้อง'};
  if(!Array.isArray(types))types=['LINK','TEXT','IMAGE','CAMERA'];
  if(!types.length)return 'ไม่ต้องส่งชิ้นงานปฏิบัติ';
  return types.map(function(t){return labels[t]||t;}).join(', ');
}
function renderLearningActivitiesTable(){
  var body=document.getElementById('learningActivitiesTableBody');if(!body)return;
  if(!globalLearningActivities.length){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="10" class="p-5 text-center text-slate-400">ยังไม่มีกิจกรรมการเรียนการสอน กรอกแบบฟอร์มด้านบนเพื่อเพิ่มกิจกรรม</td></tr>';return;}
  body.innerHTML=globalLearningActivities.map(function(a){var badge=a.status==='เผยแพร่'?'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800':a.status==='ปิดกิจกรรม'?'bg-red-100 text-red-800':'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700';return '<tr class="align-top"><td class="p-2 border font-semibold">'+esc(a.title)+'<div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-normal mt-1">'+esc(a.details||'')+'</div></td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(a.subjectCode+' - '+a.subjectName)+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center font-semibold">'+esc(a.week?'สัปดาห์ที่ '+a.week:'-')+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(formatActivityQuizLabel(a))+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(activitySubmissionLabelClient(a.submissionTypes))+'</td><td class="p-2 border whitespace-nowrap">'+esc(formatActivityDateBEClient(a.startDate))+'</td><td class="p-2 border whitespace-nowrap">'+esc(formatActivityDateBEClient(a.endDate))+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(a.targetLabel||activityTargetLabelClient(a))+'</td><td class="p-2 border"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded '+badge+'">'+esc(a.status)+'</span></td><td class="p-2 border whitespace-nowrap"><button type="button" onclick="editLearningActivity(\\''+esc(a.activityId)+'\\')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-amber-100 rounded">แก้ไข</button> <button type="button" onclick="deleteLearningActivity(\\''+esc(a.activityId)+'\\')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-red-100 text-red-700 rounded">ลบ</button></td></tr>';}).join('');
}
function activityTargetLabelClient(a){if(a.targetType==='LEVEL')return 'ระดับชั้น '+a.targetGrade;if(a.targetType==='ROOM')return normalizeRoomValueClient(a.targetRoom);if(a.targetType==='STUDENT')return 'นักเรียน '+a.targetStudentId;return '-';}
function normalizeRoomValueClient(value){
  var text=String(value==null?'':value).trim();
  var jsDate=text.match(/^(?:Sun|Mon|Tue|Wed|Thu|Fri|Sat)\\s+(Jan|Feb|Mar|Apr|May|Jun|Jul|Aug|Sep|Oct|Nov|Dec)\\s+(\\d{1,2})\\s+(\\d{4})/i);
  if(jsDate){var months={Jan:1,Feb:2,Mar:3,Apr:4,May:5,Jun:6,Jul:7,Aug:8,Sep:9,Oct:10,Nov:11,Dec:12};return (months[jsDate[1]]||'')+'/'+Number(jsDate[2]);}
  var ratio=text.match(/^(\\d{1,2})\\s*\\/\\s*(\\d{1,2})(?:\\s*\\/\\s*(?:19|20|25|26)\\d{2})?$/);
  return ratio?Number(ratio[1])+'/'+Number(ratio[2]):text;
}
function resetActivityForm(){
  editingActivityId='';document.getElementById('activityIdInput').value='';document.getElementById('activityForm').reset();
  var today=new Date(),year=today.getFullYear()+543,day=String(today.getDate()).padStart(2,'0'),month=String(today.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0');
  var thaiKey=day+'/'+month+'/'+year;
  document.getElementById('activityStartDate').value=thaiKey;document.getElementById('activityEndDate').value=thaiKey;
  document.getElementById('activityWeekSelect').value='';var projectTitleSelect=document.getElementById('activityProjectTitleSelect');if(projectTitleSelect)projectTitleSelect.value='';
  document.getElementById('btnSaveActivity').innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk mr-1"></i>บันทึกกิจกรรม';
  updateActivityQuizControls();updateActivityTargetControls();updateActivitySubmissionControls();
}
function editLearningActivity(activityId){
  var a=globalLearningActivities.find(function(x){return x.activityId===activityId;});if(!a)return;
  editingActivityId=a.activityId;document.getElementById('activityIdInput').value=a.activityId;document.getElementById('activitySubjectSelect').value=a.subjectCode;
  pendingActivityTarget={subjectCode:a.subjectCode,targetGrade:a.targetGrade,targetRoom:a.targetRoom,targetStudentId:a.targetStudentId};pendingActivityWeek=a.week||'';
  loadActivityFormOptions();document.getElementById('activityTitleInput').value=a.title;var projectTitleSelect=document.getElementById('activityProjectTitleSelect');if(projectTitleSelect)projectTitleSelect.value=a.title;document.getElementById('activityWeekSelect').value=a.week||'';document.getElementById('activityDetailsInput').value=a.details;
  document.getElementById('activityStartDate').value=formatActivityDateBEClient(a.startDate);document.getElementById('activityEndDate').value=formatActivityDateBEClient(a.endDate);document.getElementById('activityStatusSelect').value=a.status;
  document.getElementById('activityQuizModeSelect').value=a.quizMode||'NONE';populateActivityWeekDropdown(a.week);document.getElementById('activityQuestionNosInput').value=(a.questionNos||[]).join(',');document.getElementById('activityRandomCountInput').value=a.randomCount||'';
  document.getElementById('activityTargetTypeSelect').value=a.targetType||'LEVEL';
  var savedSubmissionTypes=Array.isArray(a.submissionTypes)?a.submissionTypes:['LINK','TEXT','IMAGE','CAMERA'];
  document.getElementById('activityNoSubmission').checked=savedSubmissionTypes.length===0;
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.activity-submission-type'),function(el){el.checked=savedSubmissionTypes.indexOf(el.value)>=0;});
  updateActivityQuizControls();updateActivityTargetControls();updateActivitySubmissionControls();
  document.getElementById('btnSaveActivity').innerHTML='<i class="fa-solid fa-floppy-disk mr-1"></i>บันทึกการแก้ไข';
  document.getElementById('activityForm').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});
}
function deleteLearningActivity(activityId){
  var a=globalLearningActivities.find(function(x){return x.activityId===activityId;});
  Swal.fire({icon:'warning',title:'ยืนยันลบกิจกรรม?',text:a?a.title:'กิจกรรมนี้',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'ลบกิจกรรม',cancelButtonText:'ยกเลิก'}).then(function(result){if(!result.isConfirmed)return;google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(res&&res.success){Swal.fire('ลบสำเร็จ',res.message,'success');if(editingActivityId===activityId)resetActivityForm();loadLearningActivities();}else showError(res&&res.message||'ลบกิจกรรมไม่สำเร็จ');}).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);}).apiDeleteLearningActivity(currentUser,activityId);});
}
document.getElementById('activityForm').addEventListener('submit',function(e){
  e.preventDefault();var mode=document.getElementById('activityQuizModeSelect').value,targetType=document.getElementById('activityTargetTypeSelect').value;
  var startText=document.getElementById('activityStartDate').value,endText=document.getElementById('activityEndDate').value;
  var form={activityId:editingActivityId,subjectCode:document.getElementById('activitySubjectSelect').value,title:document.getElementById('activityTitleInput').value.trim(),method:'',details:document.getElementById('activityDetailsInput').value,week:document.getElementById('activityWeekSelect').value,quizMode:mode,questionNos:document.getElementById('activityQuestionNosInput').value,randomCount:document.getElementById('activityRandomCountInput').value,startDate:thaiDateInputToISOClient(startText),endDate:thaiDateInputToISOClient(endText),targetType:targetType,targetGrade:document.getElementById('activityGradeSelect').value,targetRoom:document.getElementById('activityRoomSelect').value,targetStudentId:document.getElementById('activityStudentSelect').value,status:document.getElementById('activityStatusSelect').value,noPracticeSubmission:document.getElementById('activityNoSubmission').checked,submissionTypes:selectedActivitySubmissionTypes()};
  if(!form.subjectCode){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกรายวิชา','','warning');return;}
  if(!form.noPracticeSubmission&&!form.submissionTypes.length){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกรูปแบบการส่งชิ้นงาน','เลือกอย่างน้อยหนึ่งรูปแบบ หรือเลือกว่าสัปดาห์นี้ไม่ต้องส่งชิ้นงานปฏิบัติ','warning');return;}
  if(!form.week||Number(form.week)<1||Number(form.week)>52||!Number.isInteger(Number(form.week))){Swal.fire('กรุณาระบุสัปดาห์ที่เป็นตัวเลข 1-52','','warning');return;}
  if(!form.startDate||!form.endDate){Swal.fire('รูปแบบวันที่ไม่ถูกต้อง','กรุณากรอกวันที่เป็น วัน/เดือน/ปี พ.ศ. เช่น 17/10/2569','warning');return;}
  if(targetType==='LEVEL'&&!form.targetGrade||targetType==='ROOM'&&!form.targetRoom||targetType==='STUDENT'&&!form.targetStudentId){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกกลุ่มเป้าหมายให้ครบ','','warning');return;}
  Swal.fire({title:'กำลังบันทึกกิจกรรม...',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){Swal.close();if(res&&res.success){Swal.fire('บันทึกสำเร็จ',res.message,'success');resetActivityForm();loadLearningActivities();if(currentUser.role==='Student')loadStudentLearningActivities();}else showError(res&&res.message||'บันทึกกิจกรรมไม่สำเร็จ');}).withFailureHandler(function(err){Swal.close();showError(err.message||err);}).apiSaveLearningActivity(currentUser,form);
});
function studentActivityTodayKey(){var d=new Date();return String(d.getFullYear())+'-'+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');}
function isStudentActivityLive(a){var today=studentActivityTodayKey();return a.status==='เผยแพร่'&&a.startDate<=today&&today<=a.endDate;}
function courseActivityKey(teacherId,subjectCode){return String(teacherId||'')+'|'+String(subjectCode||'').toLowerCase();}
function getStudentCourseActivities(teacherId,subjectCode){return globalStudentActivities.filter(function(a){return a.teacherId===String(teacherId)&&String(a.subjectCode).toLowerCase()===String(subjectCode).toLowerCase();});}
function loadStudentLearningActivities(){
  if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='Student')return;
  document.getElementById('studentActivitiesList').textContent='กำลังโหลดกิจกรรม...';
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(!res||!res.success){document.getElementById('studentActivitiesList').textContent=res&&res.message||'โหลดกิจกรรมไม่สำเร็จ';return;}globalStudentActivities=res.activities||[];restrictedActivityCourses=res.restrictedCourses||[];renderStudentLearningActivities();if(document.getElementById('quizSubjectSelect'))renderStudentCourseOptions();}).withFailureHandler(function(e){document.getElementById('studentActivitiesList').textContent='โหลดกิจกรรมไม่สำเร็จ: '+(e.message||e);}).apiGetStudentLearningActivities(currentUser);
}
function toggleActivityWorkFields(id){var type=document.getElementById('aw-type-'+id);if(!type)return;var val=type.value;['link','text','image','camera'].forEach(function(x){var el=document.getElementById('aw-'+x+'-'+id);if(el)el.classList.toggle('hidden',val!==x.toUpperCase()&&!(x==='image'&&val==='IMAGE')&&!(x==='camera'&&val==='CAMERA'));});}
function studentActivityWorkForm(a,live){
  if(!live)return '<p class="text-xs text-slate-500">ระบบเปิดรับส่งชิ้นงานเฉพาะช่วงวันที่กำหนด</p>';
  var id=String(a.activityId||'').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g,'');if(!id)return '';
  var types=Array.isArray(a.submissionTypes)?a.submissionTypes:['LINK','TEXT','IMAGE','CAMERA'];
  types=types.filter(function(t){return ['LINK','TEXT','IMAGE','CAMERA'].indexOf(t)>=0;});
  var sub=a.submission;
  var submitted=sub?'<div class="p-2 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded text-xs text-emerald-800">ส่งชิ้นงานแล้วเมื่อ '+esc(sub.submittedAt||'-')+' · สามารถส่งแก้ไขได้ก่อนสิ้นสุดกิจกรรม'+(sub.url?'<div class="mt-1"><a href="'+esc(sub.url)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="underline">เปิดชิ้นงานที่ส่งไว้</a></div>':'')+'</div>':'';
  if(!types.length)return '<div class="mt-3 border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 rounded-lg p-3 space-y-2"><div class="font-semibold text-sm text-slate-700">สัปดาห์นี้ไม่ต้องส่งชิ้นงานปฏิบัติ</div><p class="text-xs text-slate-600">ครูผู้สอนกำหนดให้ทำกิจกรรมสัปดาห์นี้โดยไม่ต้องส่งชิ้นงาน'+(sub?'. ข้อมูลการส่งงานเดิมยังคงเก็บไว้':'')+'</p>'+submitted+'</div>';
  var labels={LINK:'ลิงก์จากเว็บไซต์',TEXT:'ข้อความแบบพิมพ์',IMAGE:'อัปโหลดรูปภาพจากเครื่อง',CAMERA:'ถ่ายภาพจากกล้อง'};
  var options=types.map(function(t,i){return '<option value="'+t+'">'+(i+1)+'. '+labels[t]+'</option>';}).join('');
  var fields=types.map(function(t){
    var lower=t.toLowerCase(),hidden=t===types[0]?'':' class="hidden"';
    if(t==='LINK')return '<div id="aw-link-'+id+'"'+hidden+'><label class="block text-xs mb-1">ลิงก์เว็บไซต์</label><input type="url" id="aw-url-'+id+'" placeholder="https://..." class="w-full p-2 border rounded text-xs bg-white"></div>';
    if(t==='TEXT')return '<div id="aw-text-'+id+'"'+hidden+'><label class="block text-xs mb-1">คำตอบ / อธิบายชิ้นงาน</label><textarea id="aw-content-'+id+'" rows="4" maxlength="12000" placeholder="พิมพ์คำตอบของนักเรียนที่นี่" class="w-full p-2 border rounded text-xs bg-white"></textarea></div>';
    if(t==='IMAGE')return '<div id="aw-image-'+id+'"'+hidden+'><label class="block text-xs mb-1">เลือกรูปภาพจากเครื่อง (สูงสุด 4 MB)</label><input type="file" id="aw-file-'+id+'" accept="image/*" class="w-full p-2 border rounded text-xs bg-white"></div>';
    return '<div id="aw-camera-'+id+'"'+hidden+'><label class="block text-xs mb-1">เปิดกล้องเพื่อถ่ายภาพ (สูงสุด 4 MB)</label><input type="file" id="aw-camera-file-'+id+'" accept="image/*" capture="environment" class="w-full p-2 border rounded text-xs bg-white"></div>';
  }).join('');
  var first=types[0];
  return '<div class="mt-3 border border-indigo-100 bg-indigo-50/60 rounded-lg p-3 space-y-2"><div class="font-semibold text-xs text-indigo-900"><i class="fa-solid fa-paper-plane mr-1"></i>ส่งคำตอบ / ชิ้นงาน</div>'+submitted+'<p class="text-xs text-slate-600">ครูกำหนดให้ส่งได้: '+esc(types.map(function(t){return labels[t];}).join(', '))+'</p><div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1" for="aw-type-'+id+'">รูปแบบคำตอบ</label><select id="aw-type-'+id+'" onchange="toggleActivityWorkFields(\\''+id+'\\')" class="w-full p-2 border rounded text-xs bg-white">'+options+'</select></div>'+fields+'<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><button type="button" onclick="submitStudentActivityWork(\\''+id+'\\')" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-bold"><i class="fa-solid fa-cloud-arrow-up mr-1"></i>'+(sub?'ส่งแก้ไข / อัปเดตชิ้นงาน':'ส่งคำตอบ / ชิ้นงาน')+'</button><span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500">ชิ้นงานจะบันทึกใน Data และแสดงให้ครูเจ้าของกิจกรรมตรวจได้</span></div><p id="aw-status-'+id+'" class="text-xs text-slate-600"></p></div>';
}
function submitStudentActivityWork(id){
  var activity=(globalStudentActivities||[]).find(function(a){return String(a.activityId).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g,'')===id;});if(!activity)return;
  var type=document.getElementById('aw-type-'+id).value,payload={activityId:activity.activityId,type:type};
  if(type==='LINK'){payload.url=document.getElementById('aw-url-'+id).value.trim();if(!payload.url){Swal.fire('กรุณากรอกลิงก์','ระบุลิงก์เว็บไซต์ก่อนส่ง','warning');return;}}
  if(type==='TEXT'){payload.content=document.getElementById('aw-content-'+id).value.trim();if(!payload.content){Swal.fire('กรุณาพิมพ์คำตอบ','กรอกข้อความก่อนส่ง','warning');return;}}
  var file=null;if(type==='IMAGE')file=(document.getElementById('aw-file-'+id).files||[])[0];if(type==='CAMERA')file=(document.getElementById('aw-camera-file-'+id).files||[])[0];
  function send(){Swal.fire({title:'กำลังส่งชิ้นงาน...',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){Swal.close();if(res&&res.success){Swal.fire('ส่งสำเร็จ',res.message,'success');loadStudentLearningActivities();lazyLoadedTargets.dashMainContent=false;var d=document.getElementById('dashMainContent'),sec=document.getElementById('sec-dashboard');if(d&&sec&&!d.classList.contains('hidden')&&!sec.classList.contains('hidden'))loadDashboardData();}else showError(res&&res.message||'ส่งชิ้นงานไม่สำเร็จ');}).withFailureHandler(function(e){Swal.close();showError(e.message||e);}).apiSubmitActivityWork(currentUser,payload);}
  if(type==='IMAGE'||type==='CAMERA'){
    if(!file){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกรูปภาพ','เลือกรูปหรือถ่ายภาพก่อนส่ง','warning');return;}
    if(!/^image\\/(jpeg|png|webp|gif)$/i.test(file.type)){Swal.fire('ชนิดไฟล์ไม่รองรับ','ใช้ไฟล์ JPG, PNG, WEBP หรือ GIF','warning');return;}
    if(file.size>4*1024*1024){Swal.fire('ไฟล์ใหญ่เกินไป','รูปภาพต้องมีขนาดไม่เกิน 4 MB','warning');return;}
    var reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=function(ev){var data=String(ev.target.result||''),comma=data.indexOf(',');if(comma<0){showError('อ่านไฟล์ภาพไม่สำเร็จ');return;}payload.fileBase64=data.slice(comma+1);payload.fileName=file.name||'activity-image.'+(file.type==='image/png'?'png':'jpg');payload.mimeType=file.type;send();};reader.onerror=function(){showError('อ่านไฟล์ภาพไม่สำเร็จ');};reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }else send();
}
function renderStudentLearningActivities(){
  var el=document.getElementById('studentActivitiesList');if(!el)return;
  if(!globalStudentActivities.length){el.innerHTML='<p class="text-sm text-slate-500">ยังไม่มีกิจกรรมที่เผยแพร่ตรงกับรายวิชาและกลุ่มเป้าหมายของคุณ</p>';return;}
  el.innerHTML=globalStudentActivities.map(function(a){var live=isStudentActivityLive(a),expired=studentActivityTodayKey()>a.endDate,upcoming=studentActivityTodayKey()<a.startDate;var status=live?'กำลังดำเนินการ':upcoming?'ยังไม่ถึงวันเริ่ม':expired?'สิ้นสุดแล้ว':'ปิดกิจกรรม';var badge=live?'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800':upcoming?'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800':'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600';var canQuiz=live&&a.quizMode!=='NONE';return '<article class="border rounded-lg p-3 space-y-2"><div class="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2"><div><h4 class="font-bold text-sm">'+esc(a.title)+'</h4><p class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(a.subjectCode+' - '+a.subjectName)+' · สัปดาห์ที่ '+esc(a.week||'-')+'</p></div><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded text-[11px] '+badge+'">'+esc(status)+'</span></div><p class="text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap">'+esc(a.details||'ไม่มีรายละเอียดเพิ่มเติม')+'</p><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-600"><span>วันที่ '+esc(formatActivityDateBEClient(a.startDate))+' ถึง '+esc(formatActivityDateBEClient(a.endDate))+'</span><span>'+esc(formatActivityQuizLabel(a))+'</span></div>'+studentActivityWorkForm(a,live)+(canQuiz?'<div><button type="button" onclick="openActivityQuiz(\\''+esc(a.teacherId)+'\\',\\''+esc(a.subjectCode)+'\\',\\''+esc(a.week)+'\\')" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded text-xs">ไปทำแบบทดสอบที่กำหนด</button></div>':'')+'</article>';}).join('');
}
function openActivityQuiz(teacherId,subjectCode,week){pendingActivityLaunch={teacherId:String(teacherId),subjectCode:String(subjectCode),week:String(week)};switchTab('test');}
function activatePendingActivityLaunch(){
  if(!pendingActivityLaunch||!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='Student')return;
  var p=pendingActivityLaunch;pendingActivityLaunch=null;var select=document.getElementById('quizSubjectSelect');
  var value=p.teacherId+'::'+p.subjectCode;var option=Array.from(select.options).find(function(o){return o.value===value;});
  if(!option){showError('ไม่พบรายวิชาที่เชื่อมกับกิจกรรมนี้');return;}
  select.value=value;onStudentSubjectChange();var weekSelect=document.getElementById('quizWeekSelect');if(Array.from(weekSelect.options).some(function(o){return o.value===p.week;})){weekSelect.value=p.week;renderQuizAtSelectedWeek();}
}




// Subject-specific note box: draft state is kept separately by subject code.
function setSubjectExamNoteStatus(text, colorClass){
  var el=document.getElementById('subjectExamNoteStatus');
  if(!el)return;
  el.className='text-[11px] '+(colorClass||'text-slate-500');
  el.textContent=text||'';
}
function markSubjectExamNoteDirty(){
  var code=document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect').value;
  if(!code)return;
  var field=document.getElementById('subjectExamNoteInput');
  subjectExamNoteDrafts[code]={text:field.value,dirty:true};
  setSubjectExamNoteStatus('มีการแก้ไขที่ยังไม่ได้บันทึก','text-amber-700');
}
function onExamSubjectChanged(){
  var select=document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect');
  if(!select)return;
  var nextCode=select.value;
  if(activeSubjectExamNoteCode && activeSubjectExamNoteCode!==nextCode){
    var oldCode=activeSubjectExamNoteCode;
    var oldDraft=subjectExamNoteDrafts[oldCode];
    var field=document.getElementById('subjectExamNoteInput');
    if(oldDraft && oldDraft.dirty){
      var oldText=field.value;
      subjectExamNoteDrafts[oldCode]={text:oldText,dirty:true};
      // บันทึกข้อความเดิมอัตโนมัติเมื่อเปลี่ยนวิชา เพื่อป้องกันข้อความสูญหาย
      saveSubjectExamNoteForCode(oldCode,oldText,true);
    }
  }
  loadSubjectExamNoteForCode(nextCode);
}
function loadSubjectExamNoteForCode(subjectCode){
  var code=String(subjectCode||'').trim();
  var field=document.getElementById('subjectExamNoteInput');
  var button=document.getElementById('btnSaveSubjectExamNote');
  if(!field)return;
  activeSubjectExamNoteCode=code;
  if(!code){
    field.value=''; field.disabled=true;
    if(button)button.disabled=true;
    setSubjectExamNoteStatus('กรุณาเลือกรายวิชาก่อน','text-slate-500');
    return;
  }
  if(button)button.disabled=false;
  var draft=subjectExamNoteDrafts[code];
  if(draft && draft.dirty){
    field.value=draft.text;
    field.disabled=false;
    setSubjectExamNoteStatus('มีข้อความที่ยังไม่ได้บันทึกของรายวิชานี้','text-amber-700');
    return;
  }
  field.disabled=true;
  setSubjectExamNoteStatus('กำลังโหลดข้อความของรายวิชา...','text-slate-500');
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
    if(activeSubjectExamNoteCode!==code || document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect').value!==code)return;
    field.disabled=false;
    if(!res || !res.success){
      field.value='';
      subjectExamNoteDrafts[code]={text:'',dirty:false};
      setSubjectExamNoteStatus(res&&res.message?res.message:'โหลดข้อความไม่สำเร็จ','text-red-600');
      return;
    }
    var text=String(res.note==null?'':res.note);
    subjectExamNoteDrafts[code]={text:text,dirty:false};
    field.value=text;
    setSubjectExamNoteStatus('โหลดข้อความของรายวิชานี้แล้ว','text-emerald-700');
  }).withFailureHandler(function(err){
    if(activeSubjectExamNoteCode!==code || document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect').value!==code)return;
    field.disabled=false;
    setSubjectExamNoteStatus('โหลดข้อความไม่สำเร็จ: '+(err.message||err),'text-red-600');
  }).apiGetSubjectExamNote(currentUser,code);
}
function saveSubjectExamNote(){
  var select=document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect');
  var code=select?select.value:'';
  var field=document.getElementById('subjectExamNoteInput');
  if(!code){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกรายวิชา','เลือกวิชาก่อนบันทึกข้อความประจำรายวิชา','warning');return;}
  if(activeSubjectExamNoteCode!==code){loadSubjectExamNoteForCode(code);}
  saveSubjectExamNoteForCode(code,field.value,false);
}
function saveSubjectExamNoteForCode(subjectCode,noteText,silent){
  var code=String(subjectCode||'').trim();
  var text=String(noteText==null?'':noteText);
  if(!code)return;
  var button=document.getElementById('btnSaveSubjectExamNote');
  if(activeSubjectExamNoteCode===code && document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect').value===code){
    setSubjectExamNoteStatus('กำลังบันทึกข้อความ...','text-slate-500');
    if(button)button.disabled=true;
  }
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
    var current=subjectExamNoteDrafts[code];
    if(current && current.text===text) subjectExamNoteDrafts[code]={text:text,dirty:!(res&&res.success)};
    else if(!current) subjectExamNoteDrafts[code]={text:text,dirty:!(res&&res.success)};
    if(activeSubjectExamNoteCode===code && document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect').value===code){
      if(button)button.disabled=false;
      if(res&&res.success){
        // หากผู้ใช้พิมพ์เพิ่มระหว่างกำลังบันทึก ให้คงสถานะยังไม่บันทึกไว้
        if(subjectExamNoteDrafts[code] && subjectExamNoteDrafts[code].dirty){
          setSubjectExamNoteStatus('มีการแก้ไขใหม่ที่ยังไม่ได้บันทึก','text-amber-700');
        }else{
          setSubjectExamNoteStatus('บันทึกข้อความของวิชานี้แล้ว','text-emerald-700');
          if(!silent)Swal.fire('บันทึกสำเร็จ',res.message||'บันทึกข้อความรายวิชาแล้ว','success');
        }
      }else{
        setSubjectExamNoteStatus(res&&res.message?res.message:'บันทึกข้อความไม่สำเร็จ','text-red-600');
        if(!silent)showError(res&&res.message||'บันทึกข้อความไม่สำเร็จ');
      }
    }else if(!(res&&res.success) && !silent){
      showError(res&&res.message||'บันทึกข้อความไม่สำเร็จ');
    }
  }).withFailureHandler(function(err){
    subjectExamNoteDrafts[code]={text:text,dirty:true};
    if(activeSubjectExamNoteCode===code && document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect').value===code){
      if(button)button.disabled=false;
      setSubjectExamNoteStatus('บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ: '+(err.message||err),'text-red-600');
    }
    if(!silent)showError(err.message||err);
  }).apiSaveSubjectExamNote(currentUser,code,text);
}




// Subject management
function loadTeacherSubjects(done){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role==='Student'){if(typeof done==='function')done();return;}google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(!res||!res.success){document.getElementById('teacherSubjectsBody').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="5" class="p-5 text-center text-red-500">'+esc(res&&res.message||'โหลดรายวิชาไม่สำเร็จ')+'</td></tr>';if(typeof done==='function')done(res);return;}globalTeacherSubjects=res.subjects||[];renderTeacherSubjects();populateSubjectDropdowns();setupTeacherConfigDropdowns();populateEnrollmentSubjects();populateTeachingPlanSubjectDropdown();populateAttendanceSubjectDropdown();if(typeof done==='function')done(res);}).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);if(typeof done==='function')done({success:false,error:e});}).apiGetTeacherSubjects(currentUser);}
function renderTeacherSubjects(){var b=document.getElementById('teacherSubjectsBody');if(!globalTeacherSubjects.length){b.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="5" class="p-5 text-center text-slate-400">ยังไม่มีรายวิชา</td></tr>';return;}b.innerHTML=globalTeacherSubjects.map(function(s,i){return '<tr><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+(i+1)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(s.subjectCode)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(s.subjectName)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(s.addedAt)+'</td><td class="p-2 border whitespace-nowrap"><button onclick="editTeacherSubject('+Number(s.rowIndex)+')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-amber-100 rounded text-xs">แก้ไข</button> <button onclick="deleteTeacherSubject('+Number(s.rowIndex)+')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-red-100 text-red-700 rounded text-xs">ลบ</button></td></tr>';}).join('');}
function populateSubjectDropdowns(){
  ['examSubjectSelect','cfgSubjectSelect'].forEach(function(id){var el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;
    if(el.tagName==='SELECT'){var old=el.value,items=filterDynamicUiOptions(id,globalTeacherSubjects,function(s){return s.subjectCode;},function(s){return s.subjectCode+' - '+s.subjectName;});el.innerHTML=items.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.value)+'">'+esc(x.label)+'</option>';}).join('')||'<option value="">-- ไม่มีรายวิชาตามตัวเลือก --</option>';if(items.some(function(x){return x.value===old;}))el.value=old;else if(items.length)el.value=items[0].value;}
    else if(el.tagName==='INPUT'||el.tagName==='TEXTAREA')el.placeholder='กรอกรหัสวิชา เช่น ว31101';
  });
  var examSelect=document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect');if(examSelect&&examSelect.value!==activeSubjectExamNoteCode)onExamSubjectChanged();else if(examSelect&&examSelect.value&&!activeSubjectExamNoteCode)loadSubjectExamNoteForCode(examSelect.value);
}
document.getElementById('subjectForm').addEventListener('submit',function(e){e.preventDefault();var code=document.getElementById('subjectCodeInput').value.trim(),name=document.getElementById('subjectNameInput').value.trim();google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r.success){Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',r.message,'success');resetSubjectForm();loadTeacherSubjects();}else showError(r.message);}).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);}).apiSaveTeacherSubject(currentUser,editingSubjectRowIndex,code,name);});
function editTeacherSubject(r){var s=globalTeacherSubjects.find(function(x){return Number(x.rowIndex)===Number(r);});if(!s)return;editingSubjectRowIndex=r;document.getElementById('subjectCodeInput').value=s.subjectCode;document.getElementById('subjectNameInput').value=s.subjectName;document.getElementById('btnSaveSubject').textContent='บันทึกการแก้ไข';document.getElementById('btnCancelSubjectEdit').classList.remove('hidden');}
function resetSubjectForm(){editingSubjectRowIndex=0;document.getElementById('subjectForm').reset();document.getElementById('btnSaveSubject').textContent='เพิ่มรายวิชา';document.getElementById('btnCancelSubjectEdit').classList.add('hidden');}
function deleteTeacherSubject(r){var s=globalTeacherSubjects.find(function(x){return Number(x.rowIndex)===Number(r);});Swal.fire({icon:'warning',title:'ยืนยันลบรายวิชา',text:'ต้องการลบ '+(s?s.subjectCode+' - '+s.subjectName:'รายวิชานี้')+' หรือไม่?',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'ลบ',cancelButtonText:'ยกเลิก'}).then(function(x){if(!x.isConfirmed)return;google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(res.success){Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',res.message,'success');loadTeacherSubjects();loadEnrollmentData();}else showError(res.message);}).apiDeleteTeacherSubject(currentUser,r);});}




// Enrollment
function loadEnrollmentData(){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role==='Student')return;google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(!res||!res.success){showError(res&&res.message||'โหลดข้อมูลนักเรียนไม่สำเร็จ');return;}globalEnrollmentData=res;populateEnrollmentSubjects();populateEnrollmentStudents();renderEnrollmentTable();}).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);}).apiGetEnrollmentData(currentUser);}
function populateEnrollmentSubjects(){var el=document.getElementById('enrollSubjectSelect');if(!el)return;var old=el.value,items=globalTeacherSubjects.length?globalTeacherSubjects:(globalEnrollmentData.subjects||[]);el.innerHTML=items.map(function(s){return '<option value="'+esc(s.subjectCode)+'">'+esc(s.subjectCode+' - '+s.subjectName)+'</option>';}).join('')||'<option value="">-- กรุณาเพิ่มรายวิชาก่อน --</option>';if(items.some(function(s){return s.subjectCode===old;}))el.value=old;}
function populateEnrollmentStudents(){var room=document.getElementById('enrollRoomSelect'),stud=document.getElementById('enrollStudentSelect');if(!room||!stud)return;room.innerHTML=(globalEnrollmentData.rooms||[]).map(function(r){return '<option value="'+esc(r)+'">'+esc(r)+'</option>';}).join('');stud.innerHTML=(globalEnrollmentData.students||[]).map(function(s){return '<option value="'+esc(s.id)+'">'+esc(s.id+' - '+s.name+' ('+s.room+')')+'</option>';}).join('');}
function renderEnrollmentTable(){var body=document.getElementById('enrollmentTableBody');if(!body)return;var code=document.getElementById('enrollSubjectSelect').value,q=(document.getElementById('enrollSearch').value||'').toLowerCase().trim();var rows=(globalEnrollmentData.enrollments||[]).filter(function(x){return x.subjectCode===code&&(!q||(x.studentId+' '+x.studentName+' '+x.room+' '+x.subjectName).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0);});if(!rows.length){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" class="p-5 text-center text-slate-400">ยังไม่มีนักเรียนในรายวิชานี้</td></tr>';return;}body.innerHTML=rows.map(function(x){return '<tr><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(x.subjectCode+' - '+x.subjectName)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(x.studentId)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(x.studentName)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(x.room)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(x.createdAt)+'</td><td class="p-2 border"><button onclick="removeEnrollment('+Number(x.rowIndex)+')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-red-100 text-red-700 rounded text-xs">นำออก</button></td></tr>';}).join('');}
function saveStudentEnrollments(){var code=document.getElementById('enrollSubjectSelect').value;if(!code){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกรายวิชา','เพิ่มรายวิชาก่อนเพิ่มนักเรียน','warning');return;}var ids=Array.from(document.getElementById('enrollStudentSelect').selectedOptions).map(function(o){return o.value;});var text=document.getElementById('enrollIdsInput').value;ids=ids.concat(text.split(/[\\r\\n,;\\t ]+/).map(function(s){return s.trim();}).filter(Boolean));var rooms=Array.from(document.getElementById('enrollRoomSelect').selectedOptions).map(function(o){return o.value;});Swal.fire({title:'กำลังเพิ่มนักเรียน...',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){Swal.close();if(res.success){document.getElementById('enrollIdsInput').value='';Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',res.message,'success');loadEnrollmentData();}else showError(res.message);}).withFailureHandler(function(e){Swal.close();showError(e.message||e);}).apiSaveStudentEnrollments(currentUser,code,ids,rooms);}
function removeEnrollment(r){Swal.fire({icon:'warning',title:'นำรายชื่อนักเรียนออก?',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'นำออก',cancelButtonText:'ยกเลิก'}).then(function(x){if(!x.isConfirmed)return;google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(res.success){Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',res.message,'success');loadEnrollmentData();}else showError(res.message);}).apiRemoveStudentEnrollment(currentUser,r);});}




// Attendance / เช็คชื่อรายวิชา
function attendanceDateIsoFromDisplay(value){
  var text=String(value||'').trim(),m,year,month,day,d;
  if(/^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$/.test(text)){m=text.split('-');year=Number(m[0]);month=Number(m[1]);day=Number(m[2]);}
  else {m=text.match(/^(\\d{1,2})[\/.-](\\d{1,2})[\/.-](\\d{4})$/);if(!m)return '';day=Number(m[1]);month=Number(m[2]);year=Number(m[3]);if(year>=2400)year-=543;}
  d=new Date(year,month-1,day);if(!year||!month||!day||d.getFullYear()!==year||d.getMonth()!==month-1||d.getDate()!==day)return '';
  return String(year).padStart(4,'0')+'-'+String(month).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(day).padStart(2,'0');
}
function attendanceDateBeDisplay(iso){var m=String(iso||'').match(/^(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})-(\\d{2})$/);if(!m)return '';return m[3]+'/'+m[2]+'/'+(Number(m[1])+543);}
function onAttendanceDateTextChanged(){markAttendanceSaveRequested();var el=document.getElementById('attendanceDateInput'),picker=document.getElementById('attendanceDatePicker'),iso=attendanceDateIsoFromDisplay(el&&el.value);if(!iso){var summary=document.getElementById('attendanceSummary');if(summary)summary.textContent='รูปแบบวันที่ไม่ถูกต้อง กรุณากรอกวัน/เดือน/ปี พ.ศ. เช่น 18/10/2569';return;}el.value=attendanceDateBeDisplay(iso);if(picker)picker.value=iso;loadAttendanceData();}
function normalizeAttendanceDateInput(){var el=document.getElementById('attendanceDateInput'),picker=document.getElementById('attendanceDatePicker');if(!el)return;var iso=attendanceDateIsoFromDisplay(el.value);if(iso){el.value=attendanceDateBeDisplay(iso);if(picker)picker.value=iso;}}
function openAttendanceDatePicker(){var picker=document.getElementById('attendanceDatePicker'),el=document.getElementById('attendanceDateInput');if(!picker)return;var iso=attendanceDateIsoFromDisplay(el&&el.value);if(iso)picker.value=iso;try{if(typeof picker.showPicker==='function')picker.showPicker();else picker.click();}catch(e){try{picker.click();}catch(ignore){}}}
function setAttendanceDateFromPicker(iso){markAttendanceSaveRequested();var el=document.getElementById('attendanceDateInput'),picker=document.getElementById('attendanceDatePicker');if(!iso)return;if(el)el.value=attendanceDateBeDisplay(iso);if(picker)picker.value=iso;loadAttendanceData();}
function initAttendanceControls(){
  var dateEl=document.getElementById('attendanceDateInput'),picker=document.getElementById('attendanceDatePicker'),weekEl=document.getElementById('attendanceWeekInput');
  if(dateEl&&!dateEl.value){var now=new Date(),iso=String(now.getFullYear())+'-'+String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');dateEl.value=attendanceDateBeDisplay(iso);if(picker)picker.value=iso;}
  else if(dateEl&&picker){var parsed=attendanceDateIsoFromDisplay(dateEl.value);if(parsed){dateEl.value=attendanceDateBeDisplay(parsed);picker.value=parsed;}}
  if(weekEl&&!weekEl.value)weekEl.value='1';
  var body=document.getElementById('attendanceTableBody');
  if(body&&!body.dataset.handlerInstalled){body.dataset.handlerInstalled='1';body.addEventListener('change',function(ev){if(ev.target&&ev.target.classList.contains('attendance-status'))updateAttendanceDraft(ev.target);});}
}
function populateAttendanceSubjectDropdown(){
  var el=document.getElementById('attendanceSubjectSelect');if(!el||!currentUser||currentUser.role==='Student')return;
  if(el.tagName==='SELECT'){
    var old=el.value,items=filterDynamicUiOptions('attendanceSubjectSelect',globalTeacherSubjects,function(x){return x.subjectCode;},function(x){return x.subjectCode+' - '+x.subjectName;});
    el.innerHTML=items.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.value)+'">'+esc(x.label)+'</option>';}).join('')||'<option value="">-- ยังไม่มี/ไม่มีรายวิชาตามตัวเลือก --</option>';
    if(items.some(function(x){return x.value===old;}))el.value=old;else if(items.length)el.value=items[0].value;
  }else if(el.tagName==='INPUT'||el.tagName==='TEXTAREA'){
    el.placeholder='กรอกรหัสวิชาที่ลงทะเบียน เช่น ว31101';
  }
  initAttendanceControls();
  if(!el.value){globalAttendanceRows=[];globalAttendanceWeeks=[];attendanceDrafts={};renderAttendanceTable();var summary=document.getElementById('attendanceSummary');if(summary)summary.textContent='กรุณาเลือกรายวิชา หรือกรอกรหัสวิชาที่ลงทะเบียน';}
}
function markAttendanceSaveRequested(){attendanceSaveRequested=true;}
function onAttendanceSubjectChanged(userInitiated){
  if(userInitiated===true)markAttendanceSaveRequested();
  var code=document.getElementById('attendanceSubjectSelect').value;
  if(!code){globalAttendanceRows=[];globalAttendanceWeeks=[];attendanceDrafts={};renderAttendanceTable();return;}
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
    if(document.getElementById('attendanceSubjectSelect').value!==code)return;
    globalAttendanceWeeks=res&&res.success?res.weeks||[]:[];
    var list=document.getElementById('attendanceWeekOptions');
    if(list)list.innerHTML=globalAttendanceWeeks.map(function(w){return '<option value="'+esc(w)+'"></option>';}).join('');
    var input=document.getElementById('attendanceWeekInput'),old=input.value.trim();
    if(input.tagName==='SELECT'){
      var weekItems=filterDynamicUiOptions('attendanceWeekInput',globalAttendanceWeeks.map(function(w){return {week:w};}),function(x){return x.week;},function(x){return x.week;});
      input.innerHTML=weekItems.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.value)+'">'+esc(x.label)+'</option>';}).join('')||'<option value="1">1</option>';
      if(weekItems.some(function(x){return x.value===old;}))input.value=old;else input.value=weekItems.length?weekItems[weekItems.length-1].value:'1';
    }else{
      // คงสัปดาห์ที่ผู้ใช้เลือกถ้ายังมีในวิชานี้; เมื่อเปลี่ยนวิชาและค่าเดิมไม่ตรงให้ใช้สัปดาห์ล่าสุดที่มีข้อมูล
      if(attendanceCurrentSubjectCode!==code && globalAttendanceWeeks.length && globalAttendanceWeeks.indexOf(old)<0){input.value=globalAttendanceWeeks[globalAttendanceWeeks.length-1];}
      else if(!old){input.value=globalAttendanceWeeks.length?globalAttendanceWeeks[globalAttendanceWeeks.length-1]:'1';}
    }
    attendanceCurrentSubjectCode=code;
    loadAttendanceData();
  }).withFailureHandler(function(err){
    globalAttendanceWeeks=[];attendanceCurrentSubjectCode=code;
    document.getElementById('attendanceSummary').textContent='โหลดรายการสัปดาห์ไม่สำเร็จ: '+(err.message||err);
    loadAttendanceData();
  }).apiGetAttendanceWeeks(currentUser,code);
}
function loadAttendanceData(){
  var subjectEl=document.getElementById('attendanceSubjectSelect'),dateEl=document.getElementById('attendanceDateInput'),weekEl=document.getElementById('attendanceWeekInput');
  if(!subjectEl||!dateEl||!weekEl)return;
  initAttendanceControls();
  var code=subjectEl.value,dateKey=attendanceDateIsoFromDisplay(dateEl.value),week=weekEl.value.trim();
  attendanceLoadedContext='';
  if(!code){globalAttendanceRows=[];attendanceDrafts={};renderAttendanceTable();return;}
  if(!dateKey){document.getElementById('attendanceSummary').textContent='กรุณาเลือกวันที่เช็คชื่อ';return;}
  if(!week){document.getElementById('attendanceSummary').textContent='กรุณาระบุสัปดาห์ที่ต้องการเช็คชื่อ';return;}
  document.getElementById('attendanceSummary').textContent='กำลังโหลดรายชื่อ สัปดาห์ที่ '+week+'...';
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
    if(!res||!res.success){globalAttendanceRows=[];attendanceDrafts={};renderAttendanceTable();document.getElementById('attendanceSummary').textContent=res&&res.message?res.message:'โหลดรายชื่อไม่สำเร็จ';return;}
    if(document.getElementById('attendanceSubjectSelect').value!==code||attendanceDateIsoFromDisplay(document.getElementById('attendanceDateInput').value)!==dateKey||document.getElementById('attendanceWeekInput').value.trim()!==week)return;
    globalAttendanceRows=res.students||[];
    attendanceLoadedContext=code+'|'+dateKey+'|'+week;
    var statusOptions=uiConfiguredOptionEntries('attendanceStatus',[{label:'มา',value:'มา'},{label:'ขาด',value:'ขาด'},{label:'ลา',value:'ลา'},{label:'สาย',value:'สาย'},{label:'โดด',value:'โดด'},{label:'กิจกรรม',value:'กิจกรรม'}]);
    var allowedStatus=['มา','ขาด','สาย','ลา','โดด','กิจกรรม'];statusOptions=statusOptions.filter(function(x){return allowedStatus.indexOf(x.value)>=0;});
    attendanceOptions=statusOptions.length?statusOptions.map(function(x){return x.value;}):(res.attendanceOptions||['มา','ขาด','สาย','ลา','โดด','กิจกรรม']);attendanceDrafts={};
    globalAttendanceRows.forEach(function(st){attendanceDrafts[st.studentId]=attendanceOptions.indexOf(st.attendance)>=0?st.attendance:(attendanceOptions[0]||'มา');});
    var oldRoom=document.getElementById('attendanceRoomSelect').value;
    setFilter('attendanceRoomSelect',res.rooms||[],'ทุกห้อง');
    if(document.getElementById('attendanceRoomSelect').tagName==='SELECT'&&(res.rooms||[]).indexOf(oldRoom)>=0)document.getElementById('attendanceRoomSelect').value=oldRoom;
    populateAttendanceStudentFilter();
    renderAttendanceTable();
  }).withFailureHandler(function(err){document.getElementById('attendanceSummary').textContent='โหลดรายชื่อไม่สำเร็จ: '+(err.message||err);}).apiGetAttendanceData(currentUser,code,dateKey,week);
}
function populateAttendanceStudentFilter(){
  var el=document.getElementById('attendanceStudentSelect');if(!el)return;
  var roomEl=document.getElementById('attendanceRoomSelect'),room=roomEl?String(roomEl.value||'').trim():'',old=el.value;
  var rows=globalAttendanceRows.filter(function(s){if(!room)return true;var r=String(s.room||'');return roomEl&&roomEl.tagName!=='SELECT'?r.toLowerCase().indexOf(room.toLowerCase())>=0:r===room;});
  el.innerHTML='<option value="">นักเรียนทุกคน</option>'+rows.map(function(s){return '<option value="'+esc(s.studentId)+'">'+esc(s.studentId+' - '+s.studentName)+'</option>';}).join('');
  if(rows.some(function(s){return s.studentId===old;}))el.value=old;
}
function getVisibleAttendanceRows(){
  var roomEl=document.getElementById('attendanceRoomSelect'),room=roomEl?String(roomEl.value||'').trim():'',studentId=document.getElementById('attendanceStudentSelect').value,q=(document.getElementById('attendanceSearch').value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  return globalAttendanceRows.filter(function(s){
    if(room){var rowRoom=String(s.room||'');if(roomEl&&roomEl.tagName!=='SELECT'){if(rowRoom.toLowerCase().indexOf(room.toLowerCase())<0)return false;}else if(rowRoom!==room)return false;}
    if(studentId&&String(s.studentId)!==studentId)return false;
    if(q&&(String(s.studentId)+' '+String(s.studentName)+' '+String(s.room)+' '+String(s.grade)).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)<0)return false;
    return true;
  });
}
function updateAttendanceDraft(el){
  var id=el&&el.getAttribute('data-student-id');if(!id)return;
  var value=(el.type==='checkbox'||el.dataset.statusMode==='checkbox')?(el.checked?'มา':'ขาด'):String(el.value||'').trim();
  if(DASH_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES.indexOf(value)<0)value='มา';
  markAttendanceSaveRequested();
  attendanceDrafts[id]=value;
  var label=el.parentElement&&el.parentElement.querySelector('[data-status-checkbox-label]');if(label)label.textContent=value;
  var code=String((document.getElementById('attendanceSubjectSelect')||{}).value||''),week=String((document.getElementById('attendanceWeekInput')||{}).value||'').trim();
  globalDashboardRows.forEach(function(row){
    if(String(row.studentId)!==String(id)||String(row.subjectCode)!==code||String(row.week).trim()!==week)return;
    row.attendance=value;
    var dashboardSelect=document.querySelector('#dashTableBody [data-dash-field="attendance"][data-row-index="'+Number(row.rowIndex)+'"]');
    if(dashboardSelect){dashboardSelect.value=value;dashboardSelect.style.cssText=dashboardAttendanceInlineStyle(value);}
  });
}
function renderAttendanceTable(){
  var body=document.getElementById('attendanceTableBody');if(!body)return;
  var rows=getVisibleAttendanceRows(),week=(document.getElementById('attendanceWeekInput')||{}).value||'';
  if(!rows.length){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7" class="p-5 text-center text-slate-400">ไม่พบรายชื่อตามเงื่อนไข หรือยังไม่มีนักเรียนในรายวิชานี้</td></tr>';var summary=document.getElementById('attendanceSummary');if(summary)summary.textContent='แสดง 0 คน จาก '+globalAttendanceRows.length+' คน · สัปดาห์ที่ '+week;return;}
  var statusCfg=(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.controlSettings||{}).attendanceStatus||{},statusType=statusCfg.type||'select';
  body.innerHTML=rows.map(function(s,i){var current=attendanceDrafts[s.studentId]||'มา',control='';
    if(statusType==='checkbox'){
      var checked=current!=='ขาด';control='<label class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 p-2 border rounded bg-white"><input type="checkbox" class="attendance-status" data-status-mode="checkbox" data-student-id="'+esc(s.studentId)+'" '+(checked?'checked':'')+'><span data-status-checkbox-label class="font-semibold">'+(checked?'มา':'ขาด')+'</span></label>';
    }else if(statusType==='text'){
      control='<input type="text" list="attendanceStatusValues" class="attendance-status w-full min-w-24 p-2 border rounded bg-white font-semibold" data-student-id="'+esc(s.studentId)+'" value="'+esc(current)+'" placeholder="มา/ขาด/สาย/ลา/โดด/กิจกรรม">';
    }else{
      var entries=uiConfiguredOptionEntries('attendanceStatus',attendanceOptions.map(function(x){return {label:x,value:x};}));entries=entries.filter(function(x){return attendanceOptions.indexOf(x.value)>=0;});if(!entries.length)entries=attendanceOptions.map(function(x){return {label:x,value:x};});var options=entries.map(function(st){return '<option value="'+esc(st.value)+'" '+(current===st.value?'selected':'')+'>'+esc(st.label)+'</option>';}).join('');
      control='<select class="attendance-status w-full min-w-24 p-2 border rounded bg-white font-semibold" data-student-id="'+esc(s.studentId)+'">'+options+'</select>';
    }
    return '<tr class="hover:bg-emerald-50"><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+(i+1)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(s.studentId)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(s.studentName)+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+esc(s.grade||'-')+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+esc(s.room||'-')+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+esc(s.week||week)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+control+'</td></tr>';
  }).join('');
  var counts={};attendanceOptions.forEach(function(x){counts[x]=0;});rows.forEach(function(s){var state=attendanceDrafts[s.studentId]||'มา';counts[state]=(counts[state]||0)+1;});
  document.getElementById('attendanceSummary').textContent='สัปดาห์ที่ '+week+' · แสดง '+rows.length+' คน จาก '+globalAttendanceRows.length+' คน · '+attendanceOptions.map(function(x){return x+' '+(counts[x]||0);}).join(' | ');
}
function saveAttendance(){
  var code=document.getElementById('attendanceSubjectSelect').value,dateKey=attendanceDateIsoFromDisplay(document.getElementById('attendanceDateInput').value),week=document.getElementById('attendanceWeekInput').value.trim(),rows=getVisibleAttendanceRows();
  if(!code){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกรายวิชา','เลือกวิชาก่อนบันทึกการเช็คชื่อ','warning');return;}
  if(!dateKey){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกวันที่','เลือกวันที่เช็คชื่อก่อนบันทึก','warning');return;}
  if(!week){Swal.fire('กรุณาระบุสัปดาห์','ระบุสัปดาห์ก่อนบันทึก เช่น 1','warning');return;}
  if(!rows.length){Swal.fire('ไม่มีรายชื่อ','ไม่มีรายชื่อนักเรียนตามตัวกรองให้บันทึก','warning');return;}
  var records=rows.map(function(s){return {studentId:s.studentId,status:attendanceDrafts[s.studentId]||'มา'};});
  Swal.fire({title:'กำลังบันทึกการเช็คชื่อ...',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){Swal.close();if(res&&res.success){Swal.fire('บันทึกสำเร็จ',res.message,'success');loadAttendanceData();loadDashboardData();}else showError(res&&res.message||'บันทึกการเช็คชื่อไม่สำเร็จ');}).withFailureHandler(function(err){Swal.close();showError(err.message||err);}).apiSaveAttendance(currentUser,code,dateKey,week,records);
}




// Dashboard and filters
function loadDashboardData(){var body=document.getElementById('dashTableBody');body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="20" class="p-5 text-center text-slate-400">กำลังโหลดข้อมูล...</td></tr>';google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){if(!res||!res.success){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="20" class="p-5 text-center text-red-500">'+esc(res&&res.message||'โหลดข้อมูลไม่สำเร็จ')+'</td></tr>';return;}globalDashboardRows=res.rows||[];dashColDefs=res.colDefs||[];dashVisibleCols=res.visibleCols||null;dashColConfig=res.config||null;applyColumnVisibility();if(currentUser.role!=='Student')renderColSettings();populateDashboardHeaderFilters();renderDashboard(globalDashboardRows);}).withFailureHandler(function(e){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="20" class="p-5 text-center text-red-500">'+esc(e.message||e)+'</td></tr>';}).apiGetDashboardData(currentUser);}
function loadKnowledgeScores(){
  if(!currentUser||currentUser.role==='Student'){showError('เมนูนี้สำหรับครูผู้สอนเท่านั้น');return;}
  var button=document.getElementById('btnLoadKnowledgeScores');if(button){button.disabled=true;button.textContent='กำลังโหลด K...';}
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
    if(button){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='โหลดคะแนน K';}
    if(!res||!res.success){showError(res&&res.message||'โหลดคะแนน K ไม่สำเร็จ');return;}
    var byRow={};(res.scores||[]).forEach(function(x){byRow[Number(x.rowIndex)]=x.scoreK;});
    var updated=0;globalDashboardRows.forEach(function(r){if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byRow,Number(r.rowIndex))){r.scoreK=byRow[Number(r.rowIndex)];updated++;}});
    populateDashboardHeaderFilters();applyDashboardFilters();
    Swal.fire({icon:'success',title:'โหลดคะแนน K สำเร็จ',text:'อัปเดตคะแนนจาก Sheet Data จำนวน '+updated+' แถว'});
  }).withFailureHandler(function(e){if(button){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='โหลดคะแนน K';}showError(e.message||e);}).apiLoadKnowledgeScores(currentUser);
}
function saveDashboardAll(){
  if(!currentUser||currentUser.role==='Student'){showError('เฉพาะครูผู้สอนเท่านั้นที่บันทึกได้');return;}
  captureDashboardDrafts();
  var byRow={};
  document.querySelectorAll('#dashTableBody [data-dash-field][data-row-index]').forEach(function(el){
    var rowIndex=Number(el.getAttribute('data-row-index')),field=String(el.getAttribute('data-dash-field')||'');
    if(!Number.isInteger(rowIndex)||rowIndex<2||!field)return;
    if(!byRow[rowIndex])byRow[rowIndex]={rowIndex:rowIndex};
    byRow[rowIndex][field]=el.value==null?'':el.value;
  });
  var records=Object.keys(byRow).map(function(k){return byRow[k];});
  if(!records.length){Swal.fire('ไม่มีข้อมูล','ไม่พบแถวนักเรียนในแดชบอร์ด โปรดลงทะเบียนนักเรียนและเผยแพร่กิจกรรมก่อน','info');return;}
  Swal.fire({title:'บันทึกข้อมูลทั้งหมด?',text:'บันทึกการมาเรียน บันทึกการจัดการเรียนรู้ และคะแนน S/A/C1/C2/C3 จำนวน '+records.length+' แถวในครั้งเดียว',icon:'question',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'บันทึกทั้งหมด',cancelButtonText:'ยกเลิก',confirmButtonColor:'#059669'}).then(function(answer){
    if(!answer.isConfirmed)return;
    var button=document.getElementById('btnDashSaveAll');if(button){button.disabled=true;button.textContent='กำลังบันทึก...';}
    Swal.fire({title:'กำลังบันทึกข้อมูล...',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      if(button){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='บันทึกทั้งหมด';}
      Swal.close();
      if(res&&res.success){
        records.forEach(function(item){
          var row=globalDashboardRows.find(function(x){return Number(x.rowIndex)===Number(item.rowIndex);});if(!row)return;
          if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item,'attendance'))row.attendance=String(item.attendance||'มา').trim()||'มา';
          if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item,'learningRecord'))row.learningRecord=item.learningRecord;
          ['scoreS','scoreA','scoreC1','scoreC2','scoreC3'].forEach(function(k){if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item,k))row[k]=String(item[k]).trim()===''?'-':Number(item[k]);});
        });
        Swal.fire({icon:'success',title:'บันทึกสำเร็จ',text:res.message||'บันทึกข้อมูลทั้งหมดเรียบร้อยแล้ว'});
        loadDashboardData();
      }else{
        Swal.fire({icon:'error',title:'บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ',text:(res&&res.message)||'ระบบไม่สามารถบันทึกข้อมูลได้'});
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err){
      if(button){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='บันทึกทั้งหมด';}
      Swal.close();showError(err&&err.message||err||'บันทึกข้อมูลไม่สำเร็จ');
    }).apiSaveDashboardBatch(records,currentUser);
  });
}
function applyColumnVisibility(){var all=dashColDefs.map(function(c){return c.key;}),vis=dashVisibleCols||all,css='';all.forEach(function(k){if(vis.indexOf(k)<0)css+='.dc-'+k+'{display:none!important;}\\n';});document.getElementById('dashColStyle').textContent=css;}
function toggleColSettings(){var el=document.getElementById('colSettingsPanel');el.classList.toggle('hidden');if(!el.classList.contains('hidden'))renderColSettings();}
function renderColSettings(){var list=document.getElementById('colSettingsList'),all=dashColDefs.map(function(c){return c.key;}),tv=(dashColConfig&&dashColConfig.teacherView)||all,sv=(dashColConfig&&dashColConfig.studentView)||all;list.innerHTML=dashColDefs.map(function(c){return '<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 p-2 border rounded bg-slate-50"><span class="text-xs">'+esc(c.label)+'</span><div class="flex gap-2 whitespace-nowrap"><label class="text-[11px] text-blue-700"><input type="checkbox" id="colT_'+c.key+'" '+(tv.indexOf(c.key)>=0?'checked':'')+'> ครู</label><label class="text-[11px] text-emerald-700"><input type="checkbox" id="colS_'+c.key+'" '+(sv.indexOf(c.key)>=0?'checked':'')+'> นักเรียน</label></div></div>';}).join('');}
function setAllCols(type,on){dashColDefs.forEach(function(c){var x=document.getElementById('col'+type+'_'+c.key);if(x)x.checked=on;});}
function saveColumnConfig(){var tv=[],sv=[];dashColDefs.forEach(function(c){var a=document.getElementById('colT_'+c.key),b=document.getElementById('colS_'+c.key);if(a&&a.checked)tv.push(c.key);if(b&&b.checked)sv.push(c.key);});google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r.success){dashColConfig=r.config;Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',r.message,'success');loadDashboardData();}else showError(r.message);}).apiSaveColumnConfig(currentUser,{teacherView:tv,studentView:sv});}
function setFilter(id,values,allLabel){var el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;var old=el.value;if(el.tagName!=='SELECT'){return;}var items=values.map(function(x){return {value:String(x),label:String(x)};});items=filterDynamicUiOptions(id,items,function(x){return x.value;},function(x){return x.label;});el.innerHTML='<option value="">'+(allLabel||'ทั้งหมด')+'</option>'+items.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.value)+'">'+esc(x.label)+'</option>';}).join('');if(items.some(function(x){return x.value===old;}))el.value=old;}
function populateDashboardHeaderFilters(){setFilter('filterDashRoom',Array.from(new Set(globalDashboardRows.map(function(r){return String(r.room||'').trim();}))).filter(Boolean).sort());setFilter('filterDashSubject',Array.from(new Set(globalDashboardRows.map(function(r){return String(r.subjectCode||'').trim();}))).filter(Boolean).sort());setFilter('filterDashWeek',Array.from(new Set(globalDashboardRows.map(function(r){return String(r.week||'').trim();}))).filter(Boolean).sort(function(a,b){return Number(a)-Number(b);}));setFilter('filterDashScoreK',Array.from(new Set(globalDashboardRows.map(function(r){return String(r.scoreK==null?'':r.scoreK).trim();}))).filter(Boolean).sort());}
function toggleDashboardSort(key){
  var next=(dashboardSortState&&dashboardSortState.key===key&&dashboardSortState.direction==='asc')?'desc':'asc';
  setDashboardSort(key,next);
}
function setDashboardSort(key,direction){
  var allowed=['no','studentId','room','grade','week','recordDate','scoreK','scoreS','scoreA'];
  if(allowed.indexOf(key)<0||['asc','desc'].indexOf(direction)<0)return;
  dashboardSortState={key:key,direction:direction};
  document.querySelectorAll('.dash-sort-toggle').forEach(function(button){
    var target=button.getAttribute('data-dash-sort-toggle');
    var active=target===key;
    button.classList.toggle('active',active);
    button.textContent=active?(direction==='asc'?'↑':'↓'):'↕';
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');
    button.setAttribute('title',active?(direction==='asc'?'กำลังเรียงจากน้อยไปมาก กดเพื่อเรียงจากมากไปน้อย':'กำลังเรียงจากมากไปน้อย กดเพื่อเรียงจากน้อยไปมาก'):'กดเพื่อเรียงจากน้อยไปมาก');
  });
  applyDashboardFilters();
}
function dashNumericStringCompare(a,b){
  var x=String(a==null?'':a).trim(),y=String(b==null?'':b).trim();
  x=x.replace(/^0+(?=\\d)/,'');y=y.replace(/^0+(?=\\d)/,'');
  if(x.length!==y.length)return x.length<y.length?-1:1;
  return x===y?0:(x<y?-1:1);
}
function dashDateSortValue(value){
  if(value instanceof Date)return isNaN(value.getTime())?null:value.getTime();
  var s=String(value==null?'':value).trim(),m;
  if(!s||s==='-' )return null;
  m=s.match(/^(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{4})(?:\\s+(\\d{1,2}):(\\d{1,2})(?::(\\d{1,2}))?)?$/);
  if(m){var d=new Date(Number(m[3]),Number(m[2])-1,Number(m[1]),Number(m[4]||0),Number(m[5]||0),Number(m[6]||0));return isNaN(d.getTime())?null:d.getTime();}
  m=s.match(/^(\\d{4})-(\\d{1,2})-(\\d{1,2})(?:[T\\s](\\d{1,2}):(\\d{1,2})(?::(\\d{1,2}))?)?/);
  if(m){var d2=new Date(Number(m[1]),Number(m[2])-1,Number(m[3]),Number(m[4]||0),Number(m[5]||0),Number(m[6]||0));return isNaN(d2.getTime())?null:d2.getTime();}
  var parsed=Date.parse(s);return isNaN(parsed)?null:parsed;
}
function dashSortInfo(row,key){
  var value=row?row[key]:null,s=String(value==null?'':value).trim();
  if(!s||s==='-'||s==='—')return {missing:true};
  if(key==='recordDate'){var dt=dashDateSortValue(value);return dt==null?{missing:true}:{missing:false,type:'date',value:dt};}
  if(key==='room'){
    var roomMatch=s.match(/^(\\d+)\\s*\\/\\s*(\\d+)$/);
    if(roomMatch)return {missing:false,type:'pair',first:Number(roomMatch[1]),second:Number(roomMatch[2]),text:s};
  }
  if(key==='studentId'&&/^\\d+$/.test(s))return {missing:false,type:'digits',value:s};
  var numericMatch=s.match(/-?\\d+(?:[.,]\\d+)?/);
  if(numericMatch){var n=Number(numericMatch[0].replace(',','.'));if(isFinite(n))return {missing:false,type:'number',value:n,text:s};}
  return {missing:false,type:'text',value:s.toLocaleLowerCase(),text:s};
}
function compareDashboardRows(a,b,key,direction){
  var av=dashSortInfo(a,key),bv=dashSortInfo(b,key);
  // ค่าว่าง/ไม่มีคะแนนให้อยู่ท้ายเสมอทั้งสองทิศทาง
  if(av.missing!==bv.missing)return av.missing?1:-1;
  if(av.missing&&bv.missing)return 0;
  var result=0;
  if(av.type==='pair'&&bv.type==='pair'){
    result=av.first===bv.first?(av.second===bv.second?0:(av.second<bv.second?-1:1)):(av.first<bv.first?-1:1);
  }else if(av.type==='digits'&&bv.type==='digits'){
    result=dashNumericStringCompare(av.value,bv.value);
  }else if((av.type==='number'||av.type==='date')&&(bv.type==='number'||bv.type==='date')){
    result=av.value===bv.value?0:(av.value<bv.value?-1:1);
  }else{
    var ax=String(av.text==null?av.value:av.text),bx=String(bv.text==null?bv.value:bv.text);
    result=ax.localeCompare(bx,'th',{numeric:true,sensitivity:'base'});
  }
  return direction==='desc'?-result:result;
}
/** เก็บค่าที่ครูกำลังแก้ในตารางลงหน่วยความจำก่อนกรอง/เรียง/โหลด K เพื่อไม่ให้ค่าที่ยังไม่กดบันทึกหายไป */
function captureDashboardDrafts(){
  if(!Array.isArray(globalDashboardRows)||!globalDashboardRows.length)return;
  var drafts={},allowed={attendance:true,learningRecord:true,scoreS:true,scoreA:true,scoreC1:true,scoreC2:true,scoreC3:true};
  document.querySelectorAll('#dashTableBody [data-dash-field][data-row-index]').forEach(function(el){
    var rowIndex=Number(el.getAttribute('data-row-index')),field=String(el.getAttribute('data-dash-field')||'');
    if(!Number.isInteger(rowIndex)||!allowed[field])return;
    if(!drafts[rowIndex])drafts[rowIndex]={};
    drafts[rowIndex][field]=el.value==null?'':String(el.value);
  });
  globalDashboardRows.forEach(function(row){
    var draft=drafts[Number(row.rowIndex)];if(!draft)return;
    Object.keys(draft).forEach(function(key){row[key]=draft[key];});
  });
}
function applyDashboardFilters(){captureDashboardDrafts();var q=(document.getElementById('dashSearch').value||'').toLowerCase().trim(),room=document.getElementById('filterDashRoom').value,sub=document.getElementById('filterDashSubject').value,week=document.getElementById('filterDashWeek').value,score=document.getElementById('filterDashScoreK').value;renderDashboard(globalDashboardRows.filter(function(r){if(room&&String(r.room)!==room)return false;if(sub&&String(r.subjectCode)!==sub)return false;if(week&&String(r.week)!==week)return false;if(score&&String(r.scoreK)!==score)return false;if(q&&(r.studentName+' '+r.studentId+' '+r.subjectName+' '+r.email+' '+r.teacherName+' '+r.studentId+' '+r.no+' '+r.room+' '+r.grade+' '+r.week+' '+r.scoreK+' '+r.scoreS+' '+r.scoreA).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)<0)return false;return true;}));}
function showVal(r,k,v){return r.hidden&&r.hidden.indexOf(k)>=0?'<span title="ครูผู้สอนไม่เปิดให้ดูข้อมูลนี้">🔒</span>':esc(v==null?'':v);}
var DASH_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES=['มา','ขาด','ลา','สาย','โดด','กิจกรรม'];
var DASH_ATTENDANCE_DEFAULT_COLORS={'มา':'#dcfce7','ขาด':'#fee2e2','ลา':'#dbeafe','สาย':'#fef3c7','โดด':'#ffe4e6','กิจกรรม':'#ede9fe'};
function dashboardAttendanceColor(value){
  var map=globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.styleOverrides&&globalUiConfig.styleOverrides.attendanceStatusColors||{};
  var color=String(map[value]||DASH_ATTENDANCE_DEFAULT_COLORS[value]||'#f1f5f9');
  return /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(color)?color.toLowerCase():'#f1f5f9';
}
function dashboardAttendanceTextColor(hex){
  var value=String(hex||'#f1f5f9').replace('#','');if(!/^[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(value))return '#334155';
  var r=parseInt(value.slice(0,2),16),g=parseInt(value.slice(2,4),16),b=parseInt(value.slice(4,6),16);
  return (0.299*r+0.587*g+0.114*b)>=155?'#1f2937':'#ffffff';
}
function dashboardAttendanceInlineStyle(value){var color=dashboardAttendanceColor(value);return 'background:'+color+';color:'+dashboardAttendanceTextColor(color)+';border-color:'+color;}
function updateDashboardAttendanceColor(select){
  if(!select)return;
  select.style.cssText=dashboardAttendanceInlineStyle(select.value);
  var tr=select.closest('[data-dashboard-row]'),rowIndex=tr?Number(tr.getAttribute('data-dashboard-row')):0;
  var row=globalDashboardRows.find(function(x){return Number(x.rowIndex)===rowIndex;});
  if(row)row.attendance=DASH_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES.indexOf(String(select.value))>=0?String(select.value):'มา';
}
function dashboardAttendanceValue(r){
  if(r.hidden&&r.hidden.indexOf('attendance')>=0) return showVal(r,'attendance',r.attendance);
  var value=String(r.attendance==null||r.attendance===''||r.attendance==='-'?'มา':r.attendance);
