  var styles={'มา':'attendance-present','ขาด':'attendance-absent','สาย':'attendance-late','ลา':'attendance-leave','โดด':'attendance-skip','กิจกรรม':'attendance-activity'};
  return '<span class="attendance-badge '+(styles[value]||'attendance-unknown')+'" style="'+dashboardAttendanceInlineStyle(value)+'">'+esc(value)+'</span>';
}
function dashboardAttendanceControlHtml(r,editable){
  if(!editable)return dashboardAttendanceValue(r);
  if(r.hidden&&r.hidden.indexOf('attendance')>=0)return showVal(r,'attendance',r.attendance);
  var current=String(r.attendance==null?'':r.attendance).trim();
  if(DASH_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES.indexOf(current)<0)current='มา';
  var options=DASH_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES.map(function(status){return '<option value="'+esc(status)+'" '+(current===status?'selected':'')+'>'+esc(status)+'</option>';}).join('');
  return '<select data-dash-field="attendance" data-row-index="'+Number(r.rowIndex)+'" aria-label="การมาเรียน '+esc(r.studentName)+'" onchange="updateDashboardAttendanceColor(this)" class="dashboard-attendance-select p-1 border rounded text-[10px] font-bold w-full min-w-0" style="'+dashboardAttendanceInlineStyle(current)+'">'+options+'</select>';
}
function activityWorkFileId(item){
  if(!item)return '';
  var direct=String(item.fileId||'').trim();if(direct)return direct;
  var urls=[String(item.url||''),String(item.previewUrl||'')];
  for(var i=0;i<urls.length;i++){
    var url=urls[i],m=url.match(/[?&]id=([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/);
    if(m)return m[1];m=url.match(/\\/file\\/d\\/([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/);if(m)return m[1];m=url.match(/\\/d\\/([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/);if(m)return m[1];
  }
  return '';
}
function activityWorkImageHtml(item,rowIndex,detail){
  var fileId=activityWorkFileId(item),url=String(item.url||item.previewUrl||''),imgClass=detail?'max-w-full max-h-[55vh] mx-auto rounded border bg-white':'max-w-[220px] max-h-[170px] object-contain rounded border bg-white mb-1';
  var link=url?'<a href="'+esc(url)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="inline-block text-blue-700 underline text-[10px] mt-1">เปิดไฟล์ต้นฉบับ</a>':'';
  if(!fileId)return '<div class="text-xs text-amber-700">ไม่พบรหัสไฟล์ภาพ</div>'+link;
  return '<div class="work-image-box"><div data-work-image-status class="text-[10px] text-slate-500 py-2">เลื่อนเมาส์มาที่ชิ้นงานเพื่อโหลดภาพตัวอย่าง…</div><img alt="ภาพชิ้นงานนักเรียน" data-work-file-id="'+esc(fileId)+'" data-work-row-index="'+Number(rowIndex)+'" class="hidden '+imgClass+'" loading="lazy"></div>'+link;
}
function loadActivityWorkImagePreviews(container){
  if(!container)return;
  var images=[];
  if(container.matches&&container.matches('img[data-work-file-id]'))images=[container];
  else if(container.querySelectorAll)images=Array.prototype.slice.call(container.querySelectorAll('img[data-work-file-id]'));
  images.forEach(function(img){
    if(img.dataset.previewLoaded==='1'||img.dataset.previewLoading==='1')return;
    var fileId=String(img.dataset.workFileId||''),rowIndex=Number(img.dataset.workRowIndex),status=img.parentElement?img.parentElement.querySelector('[data-work-image-status]'):null;
    if(!fileId||!rowIndex){if(status)status.textContent='ข้อมูลภาพไม่ครบถ้วน';return;}
    if(!currentUser||!(currentUser.role==='Teacher'||currentUser.role==='Admin')){if(status)status.textContent='เฉพาะครูเจ้าของรายวิชาเท่านั้นที่ดูภาพได้';return;}
    img.dataset.previewLoading='1';if(status)status.textContent='กำลังโหลดภาพตัวอย่าง…';
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){
      img.dataset.previewLoading='';
      if(!res||!res.success||!res.dataUrl){if(status)status.textContent=(res&&res.message)||'โหลดภาพไม่สำเร็จ คลิกเปิดไฟล์ต้นฉบับ';return;}
      var done=function(){img.classList.remove('hidden');img.dataset.previewLoaded='1';if(status)status.classList.add('hidden');};
      img.onload=done;img.onerror=function(){img.classList.add('hidden');if(status){status.classList.remove('hidden');status.textContent='แสดงภาพไม่สำเร็จ คลิกเปิดไฟล์ต้นฉบับ';}};
      img.src=res.dataUrl;if(img.complete&&img.naturalWidth>0)done();
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err){img.dataset.previewLoading='';if(status)status.textContent='โหลดภาพไม่สำเร็จ: '+String(err&&err.message||err||'กรุณาเปิดไฟล์ต้นฉบับ');}).apiGetActivityWorkImagePreview(currentUser,rowIndex,fileId);
  });
}
function activityWorkCellHtml(r){
  if(r.hidden&&r.hidden.indexOf('activityWork')>=0)return showVal(r,'activityWork',r.activityWork);
  var items=Array.isArray(r.activityWorkItems)?r.activityWorkItems:[];
  if(!items.length)return '<span class="text-slate-300">—</span>';
  var safeItems=items.filter(function(x){return x&&x.activityId!=='LEGACY';});
  if(!safeItems.length)return '<button type="button" onclick="showActivityWorkDetail('+Number(r.rowIndex)+')" class="text-xs text-indigo-700 underline">ดูข้อมูลเดิม</button>';
  var preview=safeItems.map(function(item){
    var title=esc(item.title||'ชิ้นงานนักเรียน'),type=String(item.type||'').toUpperCase(),content='';
    if(type==='IMAGE'||type==='CAMERA')content=activityWorkImageHtml(item,r.rowIndex,false);
    else if(type==='LINK')content='<a href="'+esc(item.url||'#')+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="text-blue-700 underline break-all text-xs">'+esc(item.url||'ลิงก์เว็บไซต์')+'</a>';
    else content='<span class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs">'+esc(String(item.content||'').slice(0,900))+'</span>';
    return '<div class="mb-2 last:mb-0"><div class="font-bold text-xs mb-1">'+title+'</div>'+content+'</div>';
  }).join('');
  return '<div class="work-preview-group" onmouseenter="loadActivityWorkImagePreviews(this)" onfocusin="loadActivityWorkImagePreviews(this)"><button type="button" onclick="showActivityWorkDetail('+Number(r.rowIndex)+')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-fuchsia-100 text-fuchsia-800 hover:bg-fuchsia-200 text-[10px] font-bold">'+safeItems.length+' ชิ้นงาน · ดู</button><div class="work-hover-preview">'+preview+'</div></div>';
}
function showActivityWorkDetail(rowIndex){
  var row=globalDashboardRows.find(function(r){return Number(r.rowIndex)===Number(rowIndex);});if(!row)return;
  var items=(row.activityWorkItems||[]).filter(function(x){return x&&x.activityId!=='LEGACY';});
  if(!items.length){Swal.fire({title:'กิจกรรมปฏิบัติ',text:row.activityWork||'ยังไม่มีชิ้นงาน'});return;}
  var html=items.map(function(item){var type=String(item.type||'').toUpperCase(),content='';
    if(type==='IMAGE'||type==='CAMERA')content=activityWorkImageHtml(item,rowIndex,true)+'<p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">'+esc(item.fileName||'ภาพจากนักเรียน')+'</p>';
    else if(type==='LINK')content='<a href="'+esc(item.url||'#')+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="text-blue-700 underline break-all">'+esc(item.url||'เปิดลิงก์')+'</a>';
    else content='<div class="whitespace-pre-wrap text-left border rounded p-3 bg-slate-50">'+esc(item.content||'')+'</div>';
    return '<section class="text-left border-b pb-3 mb-3"><div class="font-bold">'+esc(item.title||'กิจกรรมปฏิบัติ')+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-2">'+esc(item.studentName||row.studentName)+' · ส่งเมื่อ '+esc(item.submittedAt||'-')+'</div>'+content+'</section>';
  }).join('');
  Swal.fire({title:'ชิ้นงานของ '+esc(row.studentName),html:html,width:780,confirmButtonText:'ปิด',didOpen:function(){loadActivityWorkImagePreviews(document.querySelector('.swal2-html-container'));}});
}
function dashboardScoreInputHtml(r,key,label){
  var value=(r[key]==='-'||r[key]==null)?'':r[key];
  return '<input id="dash_'+key+'_'+Number(r.rowIndex)+'" data-dash-field="'+key+'" data-row-index="'+Number(r.rowIndex)+'" type="number" min="0" max="1000" step="any" value="'+esc(value)+'" class="w-full min-w-0 p-1 border border-slate-300 rounded text-center text-[10px] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-300" aria-label="'+esc(label)+' '+esc(r.studentName)+'">';
}
function renderDashboard(rows){
  var body=document.getElementById('dashTableBody');
  var displayRows=Array.isArray(rows)?rows.slice():[];
  if(dashboardSortState&&dashboardSortState.key)displayRows.sort(function(a,b){return compareDashboardRows(a,b,dashboardSortState.key,dashboardSortState.direction);});
  document.querySelectorAll('.dash-sort-toggle').forEach(function(button){var target=button.getAttribute('data-dash-sort-toggle'),active=!!dashboardSortState&&target===dashboardSortState.key;button.classList.toggle('active',active);button.textContent=active?(dashboardSortState.direction==='asc'?'↑':'↓'):'↕';button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');button.setAttribute('title',active?(dashboardSortState.direction==='asc'?'กำลังเรียงจากน้อยไปมาก กดเพื่อเรียงจากมากไปน้อย':'กำลังเรียงจากมากไปน้อย กดเพื่อเรียงจากน้อยไปมาก'):'กดเพื่อเรียงจากน้อยไปมาก');});
  if(!displayRows.length){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="20" class="p-5 text-center text-slate-400">ไม่พบข้อมูลตามเงื่อนไข</td></tr>';return;}
  var editable=currentUser.role==='Teacher'||currentUser.role==='Admin';
  body.innerHTML=displayRows.map(function(r,idx){
    var attendanceCell=dashboardAttendanceControlHtml(r,editable);
    var scoreSCell=editable?dashboardScoreInputHtml(r,'scoreS','คะแนนปฏิบัติ (S)'):showVal(r,'scoreS',r.scoreS);
    var scoreACell=editable?dashboardScoreInputHtml(r,'scoreA','คะแนนคุณลักษณะ (A)'):showVal(r,'scoreA',r.scoreA);
    var scoreC1Cell=editable?dashboardScoreInputHtml(r,'scoreC1','คะแนนสมรรถนะ C1'):showVal(r,'scoreC1',r.scoreC1);
    var scoreC2Cell=editable?dashboardScoreInputHtml(r,'scoreC2','คะแนนสมรรถนะ C2'):showVal(r,'scoreC2',r.scoreC2);
    var scoreC3Cell=editable?dashboardScoreInputHtml(r,'scoreC3','คะแนนสมรรถนะ C3'):showVal(r,'scoreC3',r.scoreC3);
    return '<tr data-dashboard-row="'+Number(r.rowIndex)+'" class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50"><td class="dc-no p-1 border text-center">'+showVal(r,'no',r.no||idx+1)+'</td><td class="dc-studentId p-1 border text-center">'+showVal(r,'studentId',r.studentId)+'</td><td class="dc-studentName p-1 border">'+showVal(r,'studentName',r.studentName)+'</td><td class="dc-email p-1 border">'+showVal(r,'email',r.email)+'</td><td class="dc-room p-1 border text-center">'+showVal(r,'room',r.room)+'</td><td class="dc-grade p-1 border text-center">'+showVal(r,'grade',r.grade)+'</td><td class="dc-teacherName p-1 border">'+showVal(r,'teacherName',r.teacherName)+'</td><td class="dc-subjectCode p-1 border text-center">'+showVal(r,'subjectCode',r.subjectCode)+'</td><td class="dc-subjectName p-1 border">'+showVal(r,'subjectName',r.subjectName)+'</td><td class="dc-week p-1 border text-center">'+showVal(r,'week',r.week)+'</td><td class="dc-recordDate p-1 border">'+showVal(r,'recordDate',r.recordDate)+'</td><td class="dc-attendance p-1 border text-center font-semibold">'+attendanceCell+'</td><td class="dc-learningRecord p-1 border">'+(editable?'<textarea id="rec_'+Number(r.rowIndex)+'" data-dash-field="learningRecord" data-row-index="'+Number(r.rowIndex)+'" rows="2" class="w-full min-w-0 p-1 border rounded text-[10px] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-300" aria-label="บันทึกการจัดการเรียนรู้ '+esc(r.studentName)+'">'+esc(r.learningRecord)+'</textarea>':showVal(r,'learningRecord',r.learningRecord))+'</td><td class="dc-scoreK p-1 border text-center font-bold text-blue-700">'+showVal(r,'scoreK',r.scoreK)+'</td><td class="dc-activityWork p-1 border text-center">'+activityWorkCellHtml(r)+'</td><td class="dc-scoreS p-1 border text-center">'+scoreSCell+'</td><td class="dc-scoreA p-1 border text-center">'+scoreACell+'</td><td class="dc-scoreC1 p-1 border text-center">'+scoreC1Cell+'</td><td class="dc-scoreC2 p-1 border text-center">'+scoreC2Cell+'</td><td class="dc-scoreC3 p-1 border text-center">'+scoreC3Cell+'</td></tr>';
  }).join('');
}
// Notice and quiz configuration
function saveTeacherNotice(){var n=document.getElementById('teacherNoticeInput').value.trim();if(!n){Swal.fire('คำเตือน','กรุณากรอกข้อความแจ้งเตือน','warning');return;}google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r.success)Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',r.message,'success');else showError(r.message);}).apiSaveTeacherNotice(n,currentUser);}
function loadTeacherNoticeForTeacher(){if(!currentUser||currentUser.role==='Student')return;google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r&&r.notice)document.getElementById('teacherNoticeInput').value=r.notice;}).apiGetTeacherNotice(currentUser);}
function loadStudentNoticeAndShowPopup(){refreshStudentNotice(true);}
function refreshStudentNotice(popup){var selected=document.getElementById('quizSubjectSelect'),opt=selected.options[selected.selectedIndex],chosen=opt?(opt.getAttribute('data-code')||''):'';var u=Object.assign({},currentUser,{selectedSubjectCode:chosen,selectedTeacherId:opt?opt.getAttribute('data-teacherid'):''});google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){var n=r&&r.notice?r.notice:'กรุณาทำแบบทดสอบให้ครบถ้วน';document.getElementById('studentNoticeText').textContent=n;if(popup)Swal.fire({icon:'info',title:'ข้อความแจ้งเตือนจากครูผู้สอน',text:n,confirmButtonText:'รับทราบ / เข้าทำแบบทดสอบ',confirmButtonColor:'#2563eb'});}).apiGetTeacherNotice(u);}
function setupTeacherConfigDropdowns(){
  var s=document.getElementById('cfgSubjectSelect');if(!s)return;
  var code=s.value;
  if(s.tagName==='SELECT'){var subjectItems=filterDynamicUiOptions('cfgSubjectSelect',globalTeacherSubjects,function(x){return x.subjectCode;},function(x){return x.subjectCode+' - '+x.subjectName;});s.innerHTML=subjectItems.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.value)+'">'+esc(x.label)+'</option>';}).join('')||'<option value="">ไม่มีรายวิชาตามตัวเลือก</option>';if(subjectItems.some(function(x){return x.value===code;}))s.value=code;else if(subjectItems.length)s.value=subjectItems[0].value;}
  var w=document.getElementById('cfgWeekSelect');if(!w)return;var old=w.value,tests=globalTests.filter(function(x){return String(x.subjectCode)===String(s.value);});var weeks=Array.from(new Set(tests.map(function(x){return String(x.week);}))).sort(function(a,b){return Number(a)-Number(b);});
  if(w.tagName==='SELECT'){var weekItems=filterDynamicUiOptions('cfgWeekSelect',weeks.map(function(v){return {week:v};}),function(x){return x.week;},function(x){return 'สัปดาห์ที่ '+x.week;});w.innerHTML=weekItems.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.value)+'">'+esc(x.label)+'</option>';}).join('');if(weekItems.some(function(x){return x.value===old;}))w.value=old;else if(weekItems.length)w.value=weekItems[0].value;}
  else if(w.tagName==='INPUT'||w.tagName==='TEXTAREA'){w.placeholder='กรอกหมายเลขสัปดาห์';if(!old&&weeks.length)w.value=weeks[0];}
  if(s.value&&w.value)loadQuizConfigForTeacher();
}
function toggleQuestionCountInput(){var mode=document.getElementById('cfgTypeSelect').value,el=document.getElementById('cfgCountInput'),specific=document.getElementById('cfgSpecificInput');el.disabled=mode!=='RANDOM';specific.disabled=mode!=='SPECIFIC';if(mode!=='RANDOM')el.value='';if(mode!=='SPECIFIC')specific.value='';}
function saveQuizConfig(){var code=document.getElementById('cfgSubjectSelect').value,week=document.getElementById('cfgWeekSelect').value,type=document.getElementById('cfgTypeSelect').value,count=document.getElementById('cfgCountInput').value,specific=document.getElementById('cfgSpecificInput').value;if(!code||!week){Swal.fire('คำเตือน','เลือกวิชาและสัปดาห์ที่มีข้อสอบก่อน','warning');return;}if(type==='RANDOM'&&(!count||Number(count)<1)){Swal.fire('คำเตือน','กรุณาระบุจำนวนข้อที่ต้องการสุ่ม','warning');return;}if(type==='SPECIFIC'&&!specific.trim()){Swal.fire('คำเตือน','กรุณาระบุหมายเลขข้อ เช่น 1,3,5','warning');return;}google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r.success)Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',r.message,'success');else showError(r.message);}).apiSaveQuizConfig(code,week,type,count,specific,currentUser);}
function loadQuizConfigForTeacher(){var code=document.getElementById('cfgSubjectSelect').value,week=document.getElementById('cfgWeekSelect').value;if(!code||!week)return;google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(!r||!r.config)return;document.getElementById('cfgTypeSelect').value=r.config.configType||'ALL';document.getElementById('cfgCountInput').disabled=r.config.configType!=='RANDOM';document.getElementById('cfgCountInput').value=r.config.configType==='RANDOM'?(r.config.questionCount||''):'';document.getElementById('cfgSpecificInput').disabled=r.config.configType!=='SPECIFIC';document.getElementById('cfgSpecificInput').value=r.config.configType==='SPECIFIC'?(r.config.questionNos||[]).join(','):'';}).apiGetQuizConfig(code,week,currentUser);}




// Excel-like test grid, including multi-cell paste from Excel/Sheets
function initExcelGrid(){gridRowCount=0;document.getElementById('excelGridBody').innerHTML='';addExcelRows(10);setupGridHandlers();}
function addExcelRows(n){var body=document.getElementById('excelGridBody'),html='',start=gridRowCount;for(var i=start;i<start+n;i++){html+='<tr data-row="'+i+'"><td class="excel-row-num">'+(i+1)+'</td><td><input type="number" min="1" class="grid-cell col-1" placeholder="1"></td><td><input type="number" min="1" class="grid-cell col-2" value="'+(i+1)+'"></td><td><input type="text" class="grid-cell col-3" placeholder="โจทย์คำถาม"></td><td><input type="text" class="grid-cell col-4" placeholder="ตัวเลือก 1"></td><td><input type="text" class="grid-cell col-5" placeholder="ตัวเลือก 2"></td><td><input type="text" class="grid-cell col-6" placeholder="ตัวเลือก 3"></td><td><input type="text" class="grid-cell col-7" placeholder="ตัวเลือก 4"></td><td><input type="number" min="1" max="4" class="grid-cell col-8" placeholder="1-4"></td></tr>';}body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',html);gridRowCount+=n;}
function clearExcelGrid(){initExcelGrid();}
function getGridCol(el){var c=Array.from(el.classList).find(function(x){return x.indexOf('col-')===0;});return c?Number(c.substring(4)):-1;}
function setupGridHandlers(){if(window.__kingGridHandlersInstalled)return;window.__kingGridHandlersInstalled=true;var table=document.querySelector('.excel-table'),body=document.getElementById('excelGridBody');table.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){if(e.target.classList.contains('grid-cell')){isDraggingSelection=true;selStartRow=Number(e.target.closest('tr').dataset.row);selStartCol=getGridCol(e.target);selEndRow=selStartRow;selEndCol=selStartCol;markGridSelection();}});table.addEventListener('mousemove',function(e){if(isDraggingSelection&&e.target.classList.contains('grid-cell')){selEndRow=Number(e.target.closest('tr').dataset.row);selEndCol=getGridCol(e.target);markGridSelection();}});window.addEventListener('mouseup',function(){isDraggingSelection=false;});window.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&String(e.key).toLowerCase()==='c'){var cells=document.querySelectorAll('.selected-cell');if(!cells.length)return;var r1=Math.min(selStartRow,selEndRow),r2=Math.max(selStartRow,selEndRow),c1=Math.min(selStartCol,selEndCol),c2=Math.max(selStartCol,selEndCol);if(r1===r2&&c1===c2)return;e.preventDefault();var lines=[];for(var rr=r1;rr<=r2;rr++){var vals=[];for(var cc=c1;cc<=c2;cc++){var ce=document.querySelector('tr[data-row="'+rr+'"] .col-'+cc);vals.push(ce?ce.value:'');}lines.push(vals.join(String.fromCharCode(9)));}var text=lines.join(String.fromCharCode(10));if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText)navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).catch(function(){});}});body.addEventListener('paste',handleGridPaste);}
function markGridSelection(){document.querySelectorAll('.selected-cell').forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove('selected-cell');});var r1=Math.min(selStartRow,selEndRow),r2=Math.max(selStartRow,selEndRow),c1=Math.min(selStartCol,selEndCol),c2=Math.max(selStartCol,selEndCol);if(r1===r2&&c1===c2)return;for(var r=r1;r<=r2;r++)for(var c=c1;c<=c2;c++){var el=document.querySelector('tr[data-row="'+r+'"] .col-'+c);if(el)el.classList.add('selected-cell');}}
function handleGridPaste(e){var txt=(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData).getData('text');if(!txt)return;var target=e.target;if(!target.classList.contains('grid-cell'))return;e.preventDefault();var selected=document.querySelectorAll('.selected-cell'),r0=Number(target.closest('tr').dataset.row),c0=getGridCol(target);if(selected.length){r0=Math.min(selStartRow,selEndRow);c0=Math.min(selStartCol,selEndCol);}var rows=txt.split(/\\r\\n|\\n|\\r/).filter(function(x){return x.length>0;});if(r0+rows.length>gridRowCount)addExcelRows(r0+rows.length-gridRowCount+5);rows.forEach(function(line,ri){var tr=document.querySelector('tr[data-row="'+(r0+ri)+'"]');if(!tr)return;line.split('\\t').forEach(function(v,ci){var el=tr.querySelector('.col-'+(c0+ci));if(el)el.value=v.trim();});});}
function saveExcelBatchTest(){var subject=document.getElementById('examSubjectSelect').value;if(!subject){Swal.fire('กรุณาเลือกรายวิชา','เพิ่มรายวิชาก่อนสร้างข้อสอบ','warning');return;}var list=[];document.querySelectorAll('#excelGridBody tr').forEach(function(tr,i){var get=function(c){return tr.querySelector('.col-'+c).value.trim();};var q=get(3);if(q)list.push({week:get(1),questionNo:get(2)||String(i+1),question:q,option1:get(4),option2:get(5),option3:get(6),option4:get(7),answer:get(8)||'1'});});if(!list.length){Swal.fire('คำเตือน','กรุณากรอกโจทย์อย่างน้อย 1 ข้อ','warning');return;}if(list.some(function(x){return !x.week;})){Swal.fire('คำเตือน','กรุณาระบุสัปดาห์ให้ครบทุกข้อ','warning');return;}if(list.some(function(x){return ['1','2','3','4'].indexOf(String(x.answer))<0;})){Swal.fire('คำเตือน','เฉลยต้องเป็นตัวเลข 1, 2, 3 หรือ 4','warning');return;}Swal.fire({title:'กำลังบันทึกข้อสอบ...',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){Swal.close();if(r.success){Swal.fire('บันทึกสำเร็จ',r.message,'success');clearExcelGrid();loadTestData();}else showError(r.message);}).withFailureHandler(function(e){Swal.close();showError(e.message||e);}).apiSaveBatchTestQuestions(list,currentUser,subject);}




// Test list and student quiz
function loadTestData(){google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(!r||!r.success){showError(r&&r.message||'โหลดข้อสอบไม่สำเร็จ');return;}globalTests=r.tests||[];selectedTestQuestionRows.clear();visibleTestQuestionRows=[];if(currentUser.role==='Student'){renderStudentCourseOptions();activatePendingActivityLaunch();refreshStudentNotice(false);}else{populateTestFilters();renderTeacherTestTable(globalTests);setupTeacherConfigDropdowns();}}).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);}).apiGetTestsList(currentUser);}
function populateTestFilters(){var subjects=Array.from(new Set(globalTests.map(function(t){return t.subjectCode+' - '+t.subjectName;}))).sort(),grades=Array.from(new Set(globalTests.map(function(t){return String(t.gradeLevel||'ไม่พบข้อมูล');}))).sort(function(a,b){return a.localeCompare(b,'th');}),weeks=Array.from(new Set(globalTests.map(function(t){return String(t.week);}))).sort(function(a,b){return Number(a)-Number(b);}),ans=Array.from(new Set(globalTests.map(function(t){return String(t.answer);}))).sort();setFilter('filterGradeSelect',grades);setFilter('filterSubjectSelect',subjects);setFilter('filterWeekSelect',weeks.map(function(w){return w;}));setFilter('filterAnswerSelect',ans);}
function filteredTestQuestions(){
  var val=function(id){var el=document.getElementById(id);return el?String(el.value||''):'';};
  var q=val('testSearchInput').toLowerCase().trim(),grade=val('filterGradeSelect'),sub=val('filterSubjectSelect'),week=val('filterWeekSelect'),ans=val('filterAnswerSelect');
  return globalTests.filter(function(t){
    if(grade&&String(t.gradeLevel||'ไม่พบข้อมูล')!==grade)return false;
    if(sub&&(t.subjectCode+' - '+t.subjectName)!==sub)return false;
    if(week&&String(t.week)!==week)return false;
    if(ans&&String(t.answer)!==ans)return false;
    return !q||(t.teacherName+' '+t.gradeLevel+' '+t.subjectCode+' '+t.subjectName+' '+t.question+' '+t.option1+' '+t.option2+' '+t.option3+' '+t.option4).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0;
  });
}
function applyTestFilters(){renderTeacherTestTable(filteredTestQuestions());}
function renderTeacherTestTable(rows){
  var body=document.getElementById('testTableBody');if(!body)return;
  rows=Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[];
  visibleTestQuestionRows=rows.map(function(t){return Number(t.rowIndex);});
  if(!rows.length){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="13" class="p-5 text-center text-slate-400">ไม่พบข้อสอบ</td></tr>';updateSelectedTestQuestionsUI();return;}
  body.innerHTML=rows.map(function(t){
    var n=Number(t.rowIndex),checked=selectedTestQuestionRows.has(n)?' checked':'';
    return '<tr><td class="p-2 border text-center"><input type="checkbox" aria-label="เลือกข้อสอบข้อที่ '+esc(t.questionNo)+'" onchange="toggleTestQuestionRow('+n+',this.checked)"'+checked+'></td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(t.teacherName)+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+esc(t.gradeLevel||'ไม่พบข้อมูล')+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(t.subjectCode)+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+esc(t.week)+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+esc(t.questionNo)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(t.question)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(t.option1)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(t.option2)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(t.option3)+'</td><td class="p-2 border">'+esc(t.option4)+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center">'+esc(t.answer)+'</td><td class="p-2 border text-center whitespace-nowrap"><button onclick="editTestQuestion('+n+')" class="text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800 mr-2" title="แก้ไขข้อสอบ"><i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i> แก้ไข</button><button onclick="deleteTest('+n+')" class="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800" title="ลบข้อสอบ"><i class="fa-solid fa-trash"></i></button></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
  updateSelectedTestQuestionsUI();
}
function toggleTestQuestionRow(rowIndex,checked){
  var n=Number(rowIndex);
  if(!globalTests.some(function(t){return Number(t.rowIndex)===n;}))return;
  if(checked)selectedTestQuestionRows.add(n);else selectedTestQuestionRows.delete(n);
  updateSelectedTestQuestionsUI();
}
function toggleSelectVisibleTestQuestions(checked){
  visibleTestQuestionRows.forEach(function(n){if(checked)selectedTestQuestionRows.add(n);else selectedTestQuestionRows.delete(n);});
  renderTeacherTestTable(filteredTestQuestions());
}
function clearSelectedTestQuestions(){selectedTestQuestionRows.clear();renderTeacherTestTable(filteredTestQuestions());}
function updateSelectedTestQuestionsUI(){
  var cnt=selectedTestQuestionRows.size,info=document.getElementById('testSelectedCount'),btn=document.getElementById('btnDeleteSelectedTests'),all=document.getElementById('testSelectAllCheckbox');
  if(info)info.textContent='เลือก '+cnt+' ข้อ'+(cnt>500?' (ลบครั้งละไม่เกิน 500 ข้อ)':'');
  if(btn)btn.disabled=!cnt||cnt>500;
  if(all){var selectedShown=visibleTestQuestionRows.filter(function(n){return selectedTestQuestionRows.has(n);}).length;all.checked=visibleTestQuestionRows.length>0&&selectedShown===visibleTestQuestionRows.length;all.indeterminate=selectedShown>0&&selectedShown<visibleTestQuestionRows.length;}
}
function deleteSelectedTestQuestions(){
  if(!currentUser||currentUser.role==='Student'){showError('เฉพาะครูผู้สอนเท่านั้นที่ลบข้อสอบได้');return;}
  var records=globalTests.filter(function(t){return selectedTestQuestionRows.has(Number(t.rowIndex));});
  if(!records.length){showError('กรุณาติ๊กเลือกข้อสอบก่อน');return;}
  if(records.length>500){showError('ลบได้ครั้งละไม่เกิน 500 ข้อ กรุณาลดจำนวนที่เลือก');return;}
  var items=records.map(function(t){return {rowIndex:Number(t.rowIndex),snapshot:JSON.stringify([t.teacherName,t.subjectCode,t.subjectName,t.week,t.questionNo,t.question,t.option1,t.option2,t.option3,t.option4,t.answer].map(function(v){return String(v==null?'':v).trim();}))};});
  Swal.fire({title:'ยืนยันลบข้อสอบ '+items.length+' ข้อ?',text:'การลบจะนำข้อมูลออกจากชีต Test และไม่สามารถย้อนกลับจากหน้าจอนี้ได้ กรุณาตรวจสอบรายการที่เลือก',icon:'warning',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'ยืนยันลบ '+items.length+' ข้อ',cancelButtonText:'ยกเลิก',confirmButtonColor:'#dc2626'}).then(function(confirm){
    if(!confirm.isConfirmed)return;
    Swal.fire({title:'กำลังตรวจสอบสิทธิ์และลบข้อสอบ...',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){Swal.close();if(r&&r.success){selectedTestQuestionRows.clear();Swal.fire('ลบสำเร็จ',r.message,'success');loadTestData();}else{showError(r&&r.message||'ลบข้อสอบไม่สำเร็จ กรุณาโหลดรายการใหม่');}}).withFailureHandler(function(err){Swal.close();showError(err&&err.message?err.message:err);}).apiDeleteTestQuestionsBatch(items,currentUser);
  });
}
function editTestQuestion(rowIndex){
  var t=globalTests.find(function(x){return Number(x.rowIndex)===Number(rowIndex);});
  if(!t){showError('ไม่พบข้อสอบที่ต้องการแก้ไข กรุณารีเฟรชรายการ');return;}
  var html='<div class="text-left space-y-3">'+
    '<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1">สัปดาห์</label><input id="editTestWeek" class="swal2-input" style="width:100%;margin:0" value="'+esc(t.week)+'" required></div>'+
    '<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1">โจทย์ข้อที่</label><input id="editTestQuestionNo" class="swal2-input" style="width:100%;margin:0" value="'+esc(t.questionNo)+'" required></div>'+
    '<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1">โจทย์คำถาม</label><textarea id="editTestQuestion" class="swal2-textarea" style="width:100%;margin:0;height:100px" required>'+esc(t.question)+'</textarea></div>'+
    '<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1">ตัวเลือกที่ 1</label><input id="editTestOption1" class="swal2-input" style="width:100%;margin:0" value="'+esc(t.option1)+'"></div>'+
    '<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1">ตัวเลือกที่ 2</label><input id="editTestOption2" class="swal2-input" style="width:100%;margin:0" value="'+esc(t.option2)+'"></div>'+
    '<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1">ตัวเลือกที่ 3</label><input id="editTestOption3" class="swal2-input" style="width:100%;margin:0" value="'+esc(t.option3)+'"></div>'+
    '<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1">ตัวเลือกที่ 4</label><input id="editTestOption4" class="swal2-input" style="width:100%;margin:0" value="'+esc(t.option4)+'"></div>'+
    '<div><label class="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1">เฉลย (1-4)</label><select id="editTestAnswer" class="swal2-select" style="width:100%;margin:0"><option value="1">ตัวเลือกที่ 1</option><option value="2">ตัวเลือกที่ 2</option><option value="3">ตัวเลือกที่ 3</option><option value="4">ตัวเลือกที่ 4</option></select></div>'+
    '<p class="text-xs text-slate-500">วิชาและครูผู้สอนจะคงข้อมูลเดิมไว้ การบันทึกจะแทนที่ข้อสอบในแถวเดิม</p></div>';
  Swal.fire({title:'แก้ไขข้อสอบ',html:html,width:700,showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'บันทึกทับข้อมูลเดิม',cancelButtonText:'ยกเลิก',focusConfirm:false, didOpen:function(){document.getElementById('editTestAnswer').value=String(t.answer||'1');},preConfirm:function(){var data={week:document.getElementById('editTestWeek').value.trim(),questionNo:document.getElementById('editTestQuestionNo').value.trim(),question:document.getElementById('editTestQuestion').value.trim(),option1:document.getElementById('editTestOption1').value,option2:document.getElementById('editTestOption2').value,option3:document.getElementById('editTestOption3').value,option4:document.getElementById('editTestOption4').value,answer:document.getElementById('editTestAnswer').value};if(!data.week||!data.questionNo||!data.question){Swal.showValidationMessage('กรุณาระบุสัปดาห์ หมายเลขข้อ และโจทย์ให้ครบ');return false;}return data;}}).then(function(result){if(!result.isConfirmed)return;Swal.fire({title:'กำลังบันทึกข้อสอบ...',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){Swal.close();if(res&&res.success){Swal.fire('บันทึกสำเร็จ',res.message,'success');loadTestData();}else showError(res&&res.message||'แก้ไขข้อสอบไม่สำเร็จ');}).withFailureHandler(function(err){Swal.close();showError(err.message||err);}).apiUpdateTestQuestion(rowIndex,result.value,currentUser);});
}
function deleteTest(r){Swal.fire({title:'ยืนยันลบข้อสอบ?',icon:'warning',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'ลบ',cancelButtonText:'ยกเลิก'}).then(function(x){if(!x.isConfirmed)return;google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(y){if(y.success){Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',y.message,'success');loadTestData();}else showError(y.message);}).apiDeleteTestQuestion(r,currentUser);});}
function renderStudentCourseOptions(){var el=document.getElementById('quizSubjectSelect');var active=studentCourses.filter(function(c){return c.active!==false;});var old=el.value;el.innerHTML=active.map(function(c){return '<option value="'+esc(c.teacherId+'::'+c.subjectCode)+'" data-code="'+esc(c.subjectCode)+'" data-teacher="'+esc(c.teacherName)+'" data-teacherid="'+esc(c.teacherId)+'">'+esc(c.label||c.subjectCode+' - '+c.subjectName)+'</option>';}).join('')||'<option value="">ยังไม่มีรายวิชา</option>';if(active.some(function(c){return c.teacherId+'::'+c.subjectCode===old;}))el.value=old;renderStudentQuizQuestions();}
function onStudentSubjectChange(){refreshStudentNotice(false);renderStudentQuizQuestions();}
function renderStudentQuizQuestions(){var sel=document.getElementById('quizSubjectSelect'),opt=sel.options[sel.selectedIndex],code=opt?(opt.getAttribute('data-code')||''):'',teacherName=opt?opt.getAttribute('data-teacher'):'',teacherId=opt?opt.getAttribute('data-teacherid'):'';var qs=globalTests.filter(function(t){return t.subjectCode===code&&t.teacherName===teacherName;});var courseKey=courseActivityKey(teacherId,code),restricted=restrictedActivityCourses.indexOf(courseKey)>=0,activityWeeks=getStudentCourseActivities(teacherId,code).filter(function(a){return isStudentActivityLive(a)&&a.quizMode!=='NONE';}).map(function(a){return String(a.week);});var weeks=Array.from(new Set(qs.filter(function(q){return !restricted||activityWeeks.indexOf(String(q.week))>=0;}).map(function(q){return String(q.week);}))).sort(function(a,b){return Number(a)-Number(b);});var weekEl=document.getElementById('quizWeekSelect'),old=weekEl.value;weekEl.innerHTML=weeks.map(function(w){return '<option value="'+esc(w)+'">สัปดาห์ที่ '+esc(w)+'</option>';}).join('')||'<option value="">'+(restricted?'ยังไม่มีกิจกรรมแบบทดสอบในช่วงวันที่เปิด':'ยังไม่มีข้อสอบ')+'</option>';if(weeks.indexOf(old)>=0)weekEl.value=old;renderQuizAtSelectedWeek();}
function renderQuizAtSelectedWeek(){var sel=document.getElementById('quizSubjectSelect'),opt=sel.options[sel.selectedIndex],code=opt?(opt.getAttribute('data-code')||''):'',week=document.getElementById('quizWeekSelect').value,teacherName=opt?opt.getAttribute('data-teacher'):'',teacherId=opt?opt.getAttribute('data-teacherid'):'';var all=globalTests.filter(function(t){return t.subjectCode===code&&String(t.week)===String(week)&&t.teacherName===teacherName;});var container=document.getElementById('quizQuestionsContainer'),btn=document.getElementById('btnSubmitQuiz'),courseKey=courseActivityKey(teacherId,code),restricted=restrictedActivityCourses.indexOf(courseKey)>=0;function displayQuestions(chosen){currentActiveQuizList=chosen;if(!chosen.length){container.innerHTML='<div class="p-6 text-center text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl">ไม่พบข้อสอบที่ครูกำหนด กรุณาตรวจสอบกิจกรรมการเรียนการสอน</div>';btn.classList.add('hidden');return;}container.innerHTML=chosen.map(function(q,i){return '<div class="bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border shadow-sm"><div class="font-bold mb-3">ข้อที่ '+(i+1)+'. '+esc(q.question)+'</div><div class="space-y-2">'+[1,2,3,4].map(function(n){return '<label class="flex gap-2 text-sm"><input type="radio" name="quiz_'+esc(q.questionNo)+'" value="'+n+'"><span>'+n+') '+esc(q['option'+n])+'</span></label>';}).join('')+'</div></div>';}).join('');btn.classList.remove('hidden');}if(!all.length){container.innerHTML='<div class="p-6 text-center text-slate-400 border rounded-xl">'+(restricted?'ยังไม่อยู่ในช่วงเวลาที่ครูกำหนด หรือไม่ได้รับมอบหมายแบบทดสอบนี้':'ไม่พบแบบทดสอบในรายวิชาและสัปดาห์นี้')+'</div>';btn.classList.add('hidden');currentActiveQuizList=[];return;}if(restricted){var today=studentActivityTodayKey(),plans=getStudentCourseActivities(teacherId,code).filter(function(a){return isStudentActivityLive(a)&&a.quizMode!=='NONE'&&String(a.week)===String(week);});if(!plans.length){container.innerHTML='<div class="p-6 text-center text-slate-400 border rounded-xl">แบบทดสอบนี้ยังไม่อยู่ในช่วงวันที่เปิดให้ทำ</div>';btn.classList.add('hidden');currentActiveQuizList=[];return;}var plan=plans[plans.length-1],chosen=all.slice();if(plan.quizMode==='SPECIFIC')chosen=chosen.filter(function(x){return (plan.questionNos||[]).indexOf(String(x.questionNo))>=0;});if(plan.quizMode==='RANDOM'){for(var i=chosen.length-1;i>0;i--){var j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1)),tmp=chosen[i];chosen[i]=chosen[j];chosen[j]=tmp;}chosen=chosen.slice(0,Math.min(Number(plan.randomCount)||chosen.length,chosen.length));}displayQuestions(chosen);return;}google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res){var chosen=all.slice();if(res&&res.config&&res.config.configType==='SPECIFIC'){var wanted=(res.config.questionNos||[]).map(String);chosen=chosen.filter(function(x){return wanted.indexOf(String(x.questionNo))>=0;});}else if(res&&res.config&&res.config.configType==='RANDOM'){var n=Math.max(1,parseInt(res.config.questionCount,10)||chosen.length);for(var i=chosen.length-1;i>0;i--){var j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1)),tmp=chosen[i];chosen[i]=chosen[j];chosen[j]=tmp;}chosen=chosen.slice(0,Math.min(n,chosen.length));}displayQuestions(chosen);}).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);}).apiGetQuizConfig(code,week,Object.assign({},currentUser,{selectedSubjectCode:code,selectedTeacherId:teacherId}));}
function handleStudentSubmitQuiz(e){if(e&&e.preventDefault)e.preventDefault();var sel=document.getElementById('quizSubjectSelect'),opt=sel.options[sel.selectedIndex],code=opt?(opt.getAttribute('data-code')||''):'',week=document.getElementById('quizWeekSelect').value,answers={};currentActiveQuizList.forEach(function(q){var a=document.querySelector('input[name="quiz_'+CSS.escape(String(q.questionNo))+'"]:checked');if(a)answers[q.questionNo]=a.value;});if(Object.keys(answers).length!==currentActiveQuizList.length){Swal.fire('ตอบข้อสอบไม่ครบ','กรุณาตอบทุกข้อก่อนส่ง','warning');return;}Swal.fire({title:'กำลังตรวจข้อสอบ...',allowOutsideClick:false,didOpen:function(){Swal.showLoading();}});google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){Swal.close();if(r.success){Swal.fire('ส่งข้อสอบเรียบร้อย',r.message,'success');lazyLoadedTargets.dashMainContent=false;var d=document.getElementById('dashMainContent'),sec=document.getElementById('sec-dashboard');if(d&&sec&&!d.classList.contains('hidden')&&!sec.classList.contains('hidden'))loadDashboardData();}else showError(r.message);}).withFailureHandler(function(e){Swal.close();showError(e.message||e);}).apiSubmitStudentTest(currentUser.id,code,week,answers,currentActiveQuizList.length,Object.assign({},currentUser,{selectedSubjectCode:code,selectedTeacherId:opt?opt.getAttribute('data-teacherid'):''}));}




// ---------------- ADMIN UI DESIGNER + LAZY-LOADING HELPERS ----------------
function readDirectText(el){return Array.from(el.childNodes||[]).filter(function(n){return n.nodeType===3&&String(n.nodeValue||'').trim();}).map(function(n){return String(n.nodeValue||'').trim();}).join(' ').trim();}
function setDirectText(el,value){
  if(!el)return;var nodes=Array.from(el.childNodes||[]).filter(function(n){return n.nodeType===3&&String(n.nodeValue||'').trim();});
  if(nodes.length){nodes[0].nodeValue=String(value==null?'':value);for(var i=1;i<nodes.length;i++)nodes[i].nodeValue='';}
  else if(String(value||'')){el.appendChild(document.createTextNode(String(value)));}
}
function kingCssPath(el){
  if(!el||!el.tagName)return'';
  if(el.id&&/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*$/.test(el.id))return '#'+el.id;
  var parts=[],node=el;
  while(node&&node.nodeType===1&&node!==document.documentElement){
    if(node.id&&/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*$/.test(node.id)){parts.unshift('#'+node.id);break;}
    var tag=node.tagName.toLowerCase(),parent=node.parentElement;
    if(parent){var siblings=Array.from(parent.children).filter(function(x){return x.tagName===node.tagName;});if(siblings.length>1)tag+=':nth-of-type('+(siblings.indexOf(node)+1)+')';}
    parts.unshift(tag);node=parent;
  }
  return parts.join(' > ');
}
function iconElementForUi(el){return el?el.querySelector('i,svg'):null;}
function parseUiOptionEntriesClient(raw){
  return String(raw==null?'':raw).split(/\\r?\\n/).map(function(line){line=String(line||'').trim();if(!line)return null;var i=line.indexOf('=');return i>=0?{label:line.slice(0,i).trim(),value:line.slice(i+1).trim()}:{label:line,value:line};}).filter(function(x){return x&&x.value!=='';});
}
function uiConfiguredOptionEntries(id,fallback){
  var cfg=(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.controlSettings||{})[id];
  if(cfg&&cfg.type==='select'&&String(cfg.options||'').trim())return parseUiOptionEntriesClient(cfg.options);
  return (fallback||[]).map(function(x){return typeof x==='string'?{label:x,value:x}:{label:String(x.label==null?x.value:x.label),value:String(x.value)};});
}
function filterDynamicUiOptions(id,items,getValue,getLabel){
  var source=(items||[]).map(function(item){return {source:item,value:String(getValue(item)==null?'':getValue(item)),label:String(getLabel(item)==null?'':getLabel(item))};});
  var cfg=(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.controlSettings||{})[id];
  if(!cfg||cfg.type!=='select'||!String(cfg.options||'').trim())return source.map(function(x){return {value:x.value,label:x.label,source:x.source};});
  var entries=parseUiOptionEntriesClient(cfg.options),byValue={};entries.forEach(function(x){byValue[String(x.value)]=x;});
  var filtered=source.filter(function(x){return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byValue,x.value);}).map(function(x){return {value:x.value,label:byValue[x.value].label||x.label,source:x.source};});
  return filtered.length?filtered:source.map(function(x){return {value:x.value,label:x.label,source:x.source};});
}
function adminUiTargetControlDefinitions(){
  return [
    {id:'attendanceStatus',label:'แดชบอร์ดจัดการเรียนรู้ › การมาเรียน',base:'select',options:'มา=มา\\nขาด=ขาด\\nลา=ลา\\nสาย=สาย\\nโดด=โดด\\nกิจกรรม=กิจกรรม',types:['select','checkbox','text']},
    {id:'cfgSubjectSelect',label:'ตั้งค่ารูปแบบข้อสอบ › วิชา',base:'select',options:'',types:['select','text']},
    {id:'cfgWeekSelect',label:'ตั้งค่ารูปแบบข้อสอบ › สัปดาห์',base:'select',options:'',types:['select','text']},
    {id:'cfgTypeSelect',label:'ตั้งค่ารูปแบบข้อสอบ › รูปแบบ',base:'select',options:'ทำทั้งหมดตามจำนวนที่มี=ALL\\nเจาะจงหมายเลขข้อ=SPECIFIC\\nสุ่มข้อสอบ=RANDOM',types:['select','checkbox','text']}
  ];
}




function inferredUiControlType(el){if(!el)return'text';if(el.id==='attendanceDateInput')return'date';if(el.tagName==='SELECT')return'select';if(el.tagName==='TEXTAREA')return'text';var t=String(el.type||'text').toLowerCase();if(t==='checkbox')return'checkbox';if(t==='date')return'date';return'text';}
function getUiControlOptions(el){if(!el)return'';if(el.tagName==='SELECT')return Array.from(el.options||[]).map(function(o){return String(o.textContent||'').trim()?(String(o.textContent||'').trim()+(o.value&&o.value!==o.textContent?'='+o.value:'')):'';}).filter(Boolean).join('\\n');var list=el.getAttribute&&el.getAttribute('list');var dl=list?document.getElementById(list):null;return dl?Array.from(dl.options||[]).map(function(o){return String(o.value||'');}).filter(Boolean).join('\\n'):'';}
function shouldSkipUiElement(el){
  if(!el||!el.tagName)return true;
  if(el.closest('#adminUiEditorPanel'))return true;
  if(el.closest('script,style,textarea,select,option,tbody,svg path'))return true;
  if(el.closest('#adminSheetContainer,#dashTableBody,#attendanceTableBody,#teacherSubjectsBody,#enrollmentTableBody,#learningActivitiesTableBody,#teachingPlanTableBody,#quizQuestionsContainer,#studentActivitiesList,#colSettingsList,#adminUiTextRowsBody,#adminUiControlRowsBody'))return true;
  if(el.tagName==='SCRIPT'||el.tagName==='STYLE'||el.tagName==='OPTION'||el.tagName==='INPUT'||el.tagName==='TEXTAREA'||el.tagName==='SELECT')return true;
  return false;
}
function captureKingUiOriginals(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#loginScreen *,#mainScreen *').forEach(function(el){
    if(!el.dataset.kingUiSelector)el.dataset.kingUiSelector=kingCssPath(el);
    if(el.dataset.kingOriginalDirectText===undefined){var txt=readDirectText(el);if(txt)el.dataset.kingOriginalDirectText=txt;}
    var ico=iconElementForUi(el);if(ico&&el.dataset.kingOriginalIconClass===undefined)el.dataset.kingOriginalIconClass=ico.getAttribute('class')||'';
    if((el.tagName==='INPUT'||el.tagName==='SELECT'||el.tagName==='TEXTAREA')&&el.id&&!el.dataset.kingOriginalControlType){
      el.dataset.kingOriginalControlType=inferredUiControlType(el);el.dataset.kingOriginalTag=el.tagName.toLowerCase();el.dataset.kingOriginalOptions=getUiControlOptions(el);
    }
  });
}
function applyKingUiControlSettings(){
  var settings=(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.controlSettings)||{};
  Object.keys(settings).forEach(function(id){var cfg=settings[id],el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el||el.closest('#adminUiEditorPanel')||!cfg)return;if(id==='attendanceDatePicker')return;if(id==='attendanceDateInput'){el.type='text';el.dataset.kingUiControlApplied='date';return;}applyKingUiControlSetting(el,cfg);});
}
function copyUiAttributes(oldEl,newEl){Array.from(oldEl.attributes||[]).forEach(function(a){if(['type','value','checked','rows','cols','list','multiple','size'].indexOf(a.name.toLowerCase())>=0)return;try{newEl.setAttribute(a.name,a.value);}catch(ignore){}});}
function buildUiOptions(select,oldEl,optionText){
  var lines=String(optionText||'').split(/\\r?\\n/).map(function(x){return x.trim();}).filter(Boolean),oldOptions=Array.from((oldEl&&oldEl.options)||[]);
  if(!lines.length&&oldOptions.length){oldOptions.forEach(function(o){var n=document.createElement('option');n.value=o.value;n.textContent=o.textContent;select.appendChild(n);});return;}
  lines.forEach(function(line){var at=line.indexOf('='),label=at>=0?line.slice(0,at).trim():line,value=at>=0?line.slice(at+1).trim():label;var o=document.createElement('option');o.value=value;o.textContent=label;select.appendChild(o);});
  if(!select.options.length){var empty=document.createElement('option');empty.value='';empty.textContent='-- กรุณากำหนดตัวเลือกใน Admin --';select.appendChild(empty);}
}
function applyKingUiControlSetting(oldEl,cfg){
  var wanted=String(cfg.type||'text'),oldValue=oldEl.value,wasChecked=!!oldEl.checked,oldSelectOptions=Array.from((oldEl&&oldEl.options)||[]).map(function(o){return {value:o.value,textContent:o.textContent};});
  var targetTag=wanted==='select'?'select':(wanted==='text'&&(oldEl.dataset.kingOriginalTag==='textarea'||oldEl.tagName==='TEXTAREA')?'textarea':'input');
  var mustReplace=oldEl.tagName.toLowerCase()!==targetTag;
  var el=oldEl;
  if(mustReplace){el=document.createElement(targetTag);copyUiAttributes(oldEl,el);if(targetTag==='input'){if(wanted!=='text')el.setAttribute('type',wanted);else el.setAttribute('type','text');}oldEl.parentNode.replaceChild(el,oldEl);}
  if(targetTag==='input'){el.type=wanted==='select'?'text':wanted;}
  if(targetTag==='select'){
    while(el.firstChild)el.removeChild(el.firstChild);buildUiOptions(el,{options:oldSelectOptions},cfg.options);
    if(Array.from(el.options).some(function(o){return o.value===String(oldValue);}))el.value=String(oldValue);else if(el.options.length)el.selectedIndex=0;
  }else if(targetTag==='input'&&wanted==='checkbox'){
    if(el.id==='cfgTypeSelect'){
      // ช่องรูปแบบข้อสอบแบบติ๊กถูกใช้สถานะติ๊ก=สุ่มข้อสอบ และไม่ติ๊ก=ทำทั้งหมด
      el.checked=String(oldValue)==='RANDOM'||wasChecked;
      try{Object.defineProperty(el,'value',{configurable:true,get:function(){return this.checked?'RANDOM':'ALL';},set:function(v){this.checked=String(v)==='RANDOM';}});}catch(ignore){}
    }else{
      el.checked=wasChecked||['true','1','yes','on'].indexOf(String(oldValue).toLowerCase())>=0;
      // ให้โค้ดฟอร์มเดิมที่อ่าน .value ได้ค่า true/false ตามสถานะติ๊กถูก
      try{Object.defineProperty(el,'value',{configurable:true,get:function(){return this.checked?'true':'false';},set:function(v){this.checked=['true','1','yes','on'].indexOf(String(v).toLowerCase())>=0;}});}catch(ignore){}
    }
  }else if(targetTag==='input'){
    try{if(wanted==='date'&&/^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$/.test(String(oldValue)))el.value=String(oldValue);else if(wanted==='date')el.value='';else el.value=String(oldValue==null?'':oldValue);}catch(ignore){}
  }else {el.value=String(oldValue==null?'':oldValue);}
  el.dataset.kingUiControlApplied=wanted;
}
function applyKingUiTextAndIcons(){
  var cfg=globalUiConfig||{},texts=cfg.textOverrides||{},icons=cfg.iconOverrides||{};
  Object.keys(texts).forEach(function(selector){try{var el=document.querySelector(selector);if(!el)return;if(el.dataset.kingOriginalDirectText===undefined)el.dataset.kingOriginalDirectText=readDirectText(el);setDirectText(el,texts[selector]);}catch(ignore){}});
  Object.keys(icons).forEach(function(selector){try{var el=document.querySelector(selector);if(!el)return;var val=String(icons[selector]||''),ico=iconElementForUi(el);if(val==='__REMOVE__'){if(ico)ico.remove();return;}if(!ico){ico=document.createElement('i');el.insertBefore(ico,el.firstChild);}ico.setAttribute('class',val);}catch(ignore){}});
}
function loadUiConfigAndApply(done){
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){globalUiConfig=r&&r.success&&r.config?r.config:{textOverrides:{},iconOverrides:{},controlSettings:{},styleOverrides:{attendanceStatusColors:{}}};uiConfigReady=true;try{applyKingUiControlSettings();}catch(e){console.warn('UI control settings:',e);}if(typeof done==='function')done();}).withFailureHandler(function(err){globalUiConfig={textOverrides:{},iconOverrides:{},controlSettings:{},styleOverrides:{attendanceStatusColors:{}}};uiConfigReady=true;if(typeof done==='function')done();}).apiGetUiConfig();
}
function renderAdminVisibilityDefaults(){
  if(!isDesignatedAdminClient())return;
  var d=(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.collapseDefaults)||{};
  var teacher=document.getElementById('adminTeacherCollapseDefault'),student=document.getElementById('adminStudentCollapseDefault');
  if(teacher)teacher.value=d.Teacher==='show'?'show':'hide';
  if(student)student.value=d.Student==='hide'?'hide':'show';
}
function saveAdminVisibilityDefaults(){
  if(!isDesignatedAdminClient()){showError('เฉพาะ Admin รหัสครู 1735 เท่านั้น');return;}
  var teacher=document.getElementById('adminTeacherCollapseDefault'),student=document.getElementById('adminStudentCollapseDefault');
  if(!teacher||!student)return;
  var cfg={textOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.textOverrides)||{}),iconOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.iconOverrides)||{}),controlSettings:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.controlSettings)||{}),styleOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.styleOverrides)||{attendanceStatusColors:{}}),collapseDefaults:{Teacher:teacher.value,Student:student.value}};
  saveAdminUiConfigPayload(cfg,'ค่าเริ่มต้นซ่อน/แสดงสำหรับครูและนักเรียน');
}
function toggleAdminUiEditor(){if(!isDesignatedAdminClient()){showError('เฉพาะ Admin รหัสครู 1735 เท่านั้น');return;}var panel=document.getElementById('adminUiEditorPanel');if(!panel)return;var willOpen=panel.classList.contains('hidden');panel.classList.toggle('hidden',!willOpen);if(willOpen){if(!uiConfigReady){loadUiConfigAndApply(function(){renderAdminUiTextEditor();renderAdminUiControlEditor();renderAdminAttendanceColors();renderAdminVisibilityDefaults();});}else{renderAdminUiTextEditor();renderAdminUiControlEditor();renderAdminAttendanceColors();renderAdminVisibilityDefaults();}}}
function toggleAdminUiList(id,button,renderer){var el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;var show=el.classList.contains('hidden');el.classList.toggle('hidden',!show);if(button)button.textContent=show?(id==='adminUiTextEditorRows'?'ซ่อนรายการข้อความ/ปุ่ม':'ซ่อนรายการช่องกรอก'):(id==='adminUiTextEditorRows'?'แสดงรายการข้อความ/ปุ่ม':'แสดงรายการช่องกรอก');if(show&&typeof renderer==='function')renderer();}
function renderAdminUiTextEditor(){
  if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='Admin')return;
  captureKingUiOriginals();var body=document.getElementById('adminUiTextRowsBody');if(!body)return;
  var elements=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#loginScreen *,#mainScreen *')).filter(function(el){if(shouldSkipUiElement(el))return false;return !!readDirectText(el)||!!iconElementForUi(el);});
  var seen={};elements=elements.filter(function(el){var selector=el.dataset.kingUiSelector||kingCssPath(el);if(!selector||seen[selector])return false;seen[selector]=true;el.dataset.kingUiSelector=selector;return true;});
  body.innerHTML=elements.map(function(el){var selector=el.dataset.kingUiSelector,txt=readDirectText(el),orig=el.dataset.kingOriginalDirectText||txt,ico=iconElementForUi(el),iconClass=ico?(ico.getAttribute('class')||''):'',originalIcon=el.dataset.kingOriginalIconClass||'',display=el.id?('#'+el.id):(el.tagName.toLowerCase()+' · '+selector.slice(-75));
    return '<tr data-admin-ui-text-row data-selector="'+esc(selector)+'" data-original-text="'+esc(orig)+'" data-original-icon="'+esc(originalIcon)+'" data-display="'+esc(display.toLowerCase()+' '+txt.toLowerCase())+'"><td class="p-2 border align-top"><div class="font-semibold">'+esc(display)+'</div><div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 break-all">'+esc(selector)+'</div></td><td class="p-2 border"><input data-ui-text-value value="'+esc(txt)+'" class="w-full min-w-[220px] p-2 border rounded text-xs" aria-label="แก้ข้อความ"></td><td class="p-2 border"><input data-ui-icon-value value="'+esc(iconClass)+'" placeholder="fa-solid fa-star หรือ - เพื่อลบ" class="w-full min-w-[170px] p-2 border rounded text-xs" aria-label="แก้คลาสไอคอน"></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
  var status=document.getElementById('adminUiEditorStatus');if(status)status.textContent='พบองค์ประกอบข้อความ/ปุ่ม '+elements.length+' รายการ · แก้แล้วกดบันทึกข้อความ/ไอคอน';
}
function renderAdminUiControlEditor(){
  if(!isDesignatedAdminClient())return;captureKingUiOriginals();var body=document.getElementById('adminUiControlRowsBody');if(!body)return;
  var cfgs=(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.controlSettings)||{},targets=adminUiTargetControlDefinitions(),targetIds=targets.map(function(x){return x.id;});
  var rows=[];
  targets.forEach(function(def){var el=document.getElementById(def.id),saved=cfgs[def.id]||{},base=def.base,type=saved.type||base,baseOptions=def.options,options=saved.options!==undefined?saved.options:baseOptions;
    if(el&&el.dataset&&el.dataset.kingOriginalControlType)base=def.base;
    rows.push({id:def.id,label:def.label,base:base,baseOptions:baseOptions,type:type,options:options,types:def.types,virtual:!el});
  });
  var els=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#loginScreen input[id],#loginScreen select[id],#loginScreen textarea[id],#mainScreen input[id],#mainScreen select[id],#mainScreen textarea[id]')).filter(function(el){if(targetIds.indexOf(el.id)>=0||el.id==='attendanceDatePicker')return false;if(el.closest('#adminUiEditorPanel')||el.closest('table,tbody')||el.closest('#adminSheetContainer'))return false;if(/^(aw-|adm_|dash-row-|filter-|grid-|file)/i.test(el.id))return false;if(['hidden','file','submit','button','reset','image','password'].indexOf(String(el.type||'').toLowerCase())>=0)return false;return true;});
  els.forEach(function(el){var id=el.id,base=el.dataset.kingOriginalControlType||inferredUiControlType(el),saved=cfgs[id]||{},baseOptions=el.dataset.kingOriginalOptions||getUiControlOptions(el);rows.push({id:id,label:(function(){var label=document.querySelector('label[for="'+id+'"]');if(!label){var parent=el.parentElement&&el.parentElement.parentElement;label=parent&&parent.querySelector('label');}return label?readDirectText(label):id;})(),base:base,baseOptions:baseOptions,type:saved.type||base,options:saved.options!==undefined?saved.options:baseOptions,types:['text','select','checkbox','date'],virtual:false});});
  body.innerHTML=rows.map(function(r){var display=(r.label+' '+r.id).toLowerCase(),typeOptions=r.types.map(function(t){var labels={text:'แบบเติมคำ / ข้อความ',select:'ดรอปดาวน์',checkbox:'แบบติ๊กถูก',date:'ปฏิทินวันที่'};return '<option value="'+t+'" '+(r.type===t?'selected':'')+'>'+labels[t]+'</option>';}).join('');
    var note=r.id==='attendanceStatus'?'ค่าเริ่มต้นรองรับ มา, ขาด, สาย, ลา, โดด, กิจกรรม; เลือกเฉพาะสถานะที่ต้องการแสดงได้':(r.id==='cfgTypeSelect'?'ค่ารูปแบบต้องใช้ ALL, SPECIFIC หรือ RANDOM; เมื่อใช้ติ๊กถูกจะเป็น ALL/RANDOM':(['attendanceSubjectSelect','attendanceWeekInput','attendanceRoomSelect','cfgSubjectSelect','cfgWeekSelect'].indexOf(r.id)>=0?'ตัวเลือกดรอปดาวน์ใช้กรองค่าที่ระบบดึงจากรายวิชา/สัปดาห์/ห้องจริง':''));
    return '<tr data-admin-ui-control-row data-id="'+esc(r.id)+'" data-base-type="'+esc(r.base)+'" data-base-options="'+esc(r.baseOptions)+'" data-display="'+esc(display)+'"><td class="p-2 border align-top"><div class="font-semibold">'+esc(r.label)+'</div><div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">#'+esc(r.id)+(r.virtual?' · ช่องที่สร้างตามแถวข้อมูล':'')+'</div><div class="text-[10px] text-slate-500">ชนิดเดิม: '+esc(r.base)+'</div><div class="text-[10px] text-indigo-700 mt-1">'+esc(note)+'</div></td><td class="p-2 border"><select data-ui-control-type class="w-full p-2 border rounded text-xs">'+typeOptions+'</select></td><td class="p-2 border"><textarea data-ui-control-options rows="2" placeholder="ตัวเลือกหนึ่งบรรทัดต่อค่า เช่น ชื่อที่แสดง=ค่าจริง" class="w-full min-w-[250px] p-2 border rounded text-xs">'+esc(r.options)+'</textarea></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
  var status=document.getElementById('adminUiEditorStatus');if(status)status.textContent='พบช่องกรอก '+rows.length+' รายการ รวม 7 ช่องที่ตั้งค่าไว้สำหรับระบบเช็คชื่อและรูปแบบข้อสอบ';
}




function filterAdminUiRows(containerId,query){var root=document.getElementById(containerId);if(!root)return;var q=String(query||'').toLowerCase().trim();root.querySelectorAll('tr[data-admin-ui-text-row],tr[data-admin-ui-control-row]').forEach(function(row){row.classList.toggle('hidden',!!q&&String(row.dataset.display||'').indexOf(q)<0);});}
function renderAdminAttendanceColors(){
  if(!isDesignatedAdminClient())return;
  var root=document.getElementById('adminAttendanceColors');if(!root)return;
  var saved=globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.styleOverrides&&globalUiConfig.styleOverrides.attendanceStatusColors||{};
  root.innerHTML=DASH_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES.map(function(status){var color=String(saved[status]||DASH_ATTENDANCE_DEFAULT_COLORS[status]||'#f1f5f9');if(!/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(color))color=DASH_ATTENDANCE_DEFAULT_COLORS[status];return '<label class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded border p-2 text-xs bg-slate-50"><input type="color" data-attendance-color-value="'+esc(status)+'" value="'+color+'" class="w-9 h-9 cursor-pointer"><span class="font-semibold">'+esc(status)+'</span></label>';}).join('');
}
function saveAdminAttendanceColors(){
  if(!isDesignatedAdminClient()){showError('เฉพาะ Admin รหัสครู 1735 เท่านั้น');return;}
  var colors={};document.querySelectorAll('#adminAttendanceColors [data-attendance-color-value]').forEach(function(el){var status=String(el.getAttribute('data-attendance-color-value')||''),color=String(el.value||'').toLowerCase();if(DASH_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES.indexOf(status)>=0&&/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/.test(color))colors[status]=color;});
  var cfg={textOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.textOverrides)||{}),iconOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.iconOverrides)||{}),controlSettings:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.controlSettings)||{}),styleOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.styleOverrides)||{},{attendanceStatusColors:colors}),collapseDefaults:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.collapseDefaults)||{})};
  saveAdminUiConfigPayload(cfg,'สีสถานะการมาเรียน');
}
function saveAdminUiTextConfig(){
  if(!currentUser||currentUser.role!=='Admin'){showError('เฉพาะ Admin เท่านั้น');return;}
  var cfg={textOverrides:{},iconOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.iconOverrides)||{}),controlSettings:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.controlSettings)||{}),styleOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.styleOverrides)||{attendanceStatusColors:{}})};
  document.querySelectorAll('#adminUiTextRowsBody tr[data-admin-ui-text-row]').forEach(function(row){var selector=row.dataset.selector||'',textInput=row.querySelector('[data-ui-text-value]'),iconInput=row.querySelector('[data-ui-icon-value]'),value=textInput?textInput.value:'',original=row.dataset.originalText||'';if(value!==original)cfg.textOverrides[selector]=value;var iconVal=iconInput?iconInput.value.trim():'',origIcon=String(row.dataset.originalIcon||'');if(iconVal==='-')cfg.iconOverrides[selector]='__REMOVE__';else if(iconVal&&iconVal!==origIcon)cfg.iconOverrides[selector]=iconVal;else if(iconVal===origIcon)delete cfg.iconOverrides[selector];});
  saveAdminUiConfigPayload(cfg,'ข้อความ/ไอคอน');
}
function saveAdminUiControlConfig(){
  if(!isDesignatedAdminClient()){showError('เฉพาะ Admin รหัสครู 1735 เท่านั้น');return;}
  var cfg={textOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.textOverrides)||{}),iconOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.iconOverrides)||{}),controlSettings:{},styleOverrides:Object.assign({},(globalUiConfig&&globalUiConfig.styleOverrides)||{attendanceStatusColors:{}})};
  document.querySelectorAll('#adminUiControlRowsBody tr[data-admin-ui-control-row]').forEach(function(row){var id=row.dataset.id,type=row.querySelector('[data-ui-control-type]').value,options=row.querySelector('[data-ui-control-options]').value,base=row.dataset.baseType||'text',baseOpts=row.dataset.baseOptions||'';if(type!==base||options!==baseOpts)cfg.controlSettings[id]={type:type,options:options};});
  saveAdminUiConfigPayload(cfg,'ชนิดช่องกรอก/ตัวเลือกเช็คชื่อและรูปแบบข้อสอบ');
}
function saveAdminUiConfigPayload(cfg,label){
  var status=document.getElementById('adminUiEditorStatus');if(status)status.textContent='กำลังบันทึกการปรับแต่ง...';
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(!r||!r.success){if(status)status.textContent=r&&r.message||'บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ';showError(r&&r.message||'บันทึกการปรับแต่งไม่สำเร็จ');return;}globalUiConfig=r.config||cfg;try{applyKingUiControlSettings();applyKingUiTextAndIcons();renderAdminAttendanceColors();renderAdminVisibilityDefaults();if(document.getElementById('dashTableBody')){captureDashboardDrafts();applyDashboardFilters();}}catch(ignore){}if(status)status.textContent=r.message+' ('+label+')';Swal.fire('บันทึกสำเร็จ',r.message+'\\nการเปลี่ยนแปลงจะคงอยู่หลังรีเฟรชหน้าเว็บ','success');}).withFailureHandler(function(e){if(status)status.textContent='บันทึกไม่สำเร็จ: '+(e.message||e);showError(e.message||e);}).apiAdminSaveUiConfig(cfg,currentUser);
}

// Admin panel
function loadAdminData(){if(!isDesignatedAdminClient()){showError('เฉพาะ Admin รหัสครู 1735 เท่านั้น');return;}google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(!r||!r.success){showError(r&&r.message||'โหลดข้อมูลผู้ดูแลระบบไม่สำเร็จ');return;}globalAdminData={systemStatus:r.systemStatus,infoTeacher:r.infoTeacher||[],infoStudent:r.infoStudent||[],infoSubject:r.infoSubject||[],infoEnrollment:r.infoEnrollment||[],infoActivity:r.infoActivity||[],infoTeachingPlan:r.infoTeachingPlan||[],dataSheet:r.dataSheet||[],testSheet:r.testSheet||[]};updateAdminSystemBtn(r.systemStatus);switchAdminSheet(activeAdminSheet);}).withFailureHandler(function(e){showError(e.message||e);}).apiAdminGetAllData(currentUser);}
function updateAdminSystemBtn(status){var b=document.getElementById('btnSystemToggle');if(status==='OPEN'){b.textContent='ระบบเปิดอยู่ (คลิกเพื่อปิด)';b.className='px-4 py-2 rounded font-bold text-xs bg-emerald-500 text-white';}else{b.textContent='ระบบปิดอยู่ (คลิกเพื่อเปิด)';b.className='px-4 py-2 rounded font-bold text-xs bg-red-500 text-white';}}
function toggleSystemStatus(){if(!globalAdminData)return;var next=globalAdminData.systemStatus==='OPEN'?'CLOSED':'OPEN';google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r.success){globalAdminData.systemStatus=r.systemStatus;updateAdminSystemBtn(r.systemStatus);Swal.fire('อัปเดตสำเร็จ',r.message,'success');}else showError(r.message);}).apiAdminToggleSystem(next,currentUser);}
function switchAdminSheet(name){
  if(!isDesignatedAdminClient()){showError('เฉพาะ Admin รหัสครู 1735 เท่านั้น');return;}
  activeAdminSheet=name;
  document.querySelectorAll('.admin-sheet-btn').forEach(function(b){b.className='admin-sheet-btn px-3 py-2 rounded bg-slate-100 text-xs';});
  var map={InfoTeacher:'btnAdminTeacher',InfoStudent:'btnAdminStudent',InfoSubject:'btnAdminSubject',InfoEnrollment:'btnAdminEnrollment',InfoActivity:'btnAdminActivity',InfoTeachingPlan:'btnAdminTeachingPlan',Data:'btnAdminData',Test:'btnAdminTest'};
  var activeButton=document.getElementById(map[name]);if(activeButton)activeButton.className='admin-sheet-btn px-3 py-2 rounded bg-blue-600 text-white text-xs';
  var container=document.getElementById('adminSheetContainer');if(!container)return;
  var key={InfoTeacher:'infoTeacher',InfoStudent:'infoStudent',InfoSubject:'infoSubject',InfoEnrollment:'infoEnrollment',InfoActivity:'infoActivity',InfoTeachingPlan:'infoTeachingPlan',Data:'dataSheet',Test:'testSheet'}[name];
  if(globalAdminData&&key&&Array.isArray(globalAdminData[key])){renderAdminSheetTable(name,globalAdminData[key]);return;}
  container.innerHTML='<p class="p-5 text-center text-slate-500 text-xs">กำลังโหลดข้อมูล '+esc(name)+'...</p>';
  google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){
    if(!r||!r.success){container.innerHTML='<p class="p-4 text-red-600 text-xs">'+esc(r&&r.message||'โหลดข้อมูลไม่สำเร็จ')+'</p>';return;}
    if(activeAdminSheet!==name)return;
    var raw=r.data||[];globalAdminData=globalAdminData||{};if(key)globalAdminData[key]=raw;
    renderAdminSheetTable(name,raw);
  }).withFailureHandler(function(e){if(activeAdminSheet===name)container.innerHTML='<p class="p-4 text-red-600 text-xs">'+esc(e.message||e)+'</p>';}).apiAdminGetSheetData(name,currentUser);
}
function renderAdminSheetTable(name,raw){
  var container=document.getElementById('adminSheetContainer');if(!container)return;
  if(!raw||!raw.length){container.innerHTML='<p class="p-4 text-slate-400 text-xs">ไม่มีข้อมูลในชีต '+esc(name)+'</p>';return;}
  var html='<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2"><h4 class="font-bold text-sm">ข้อมูล: '+esc(name)+'</h4><button type="button" class="collapse-toggle" onclick="toggleCollapseTargets(&quot;adminSheetTableBody&quot;,this)">แสดง</button></div><div id="adminSheetTableBody" class="hidden"><table class="text-xs border-collapse min-w-full"><thead class="bg-slate-100"><tr>';
  raw[0].forEach(function(h){html+='<th class="p-2 border">'+esc(h)+'</th>';});html+='<th class="p-2 border">จัดการ</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  raw.slice(1).forEach(function(row,i){var ri=i+2;html+='<tr>';row.forEach(function(v,c){html+='<td class="p-1 border"><input id="adm_'+ri+'_'+c+'" value="'+esc(v instanceof Date?String(v):v)+'" class="p-1 border rounded min-w-20 w-full"></td>';});html+='<td class="p-1 border whitespace-nowrap"><button onclick="saveAdminRow(&quot;'+name+'&quot;,'+ri+','+row.length+')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded">บันทึก</button> <button onclick="deleteAdminRow(&quot;'+name+'&quot;,'+ri+')" class="px-2 py-1 bg-red-600 text-white rounded">ลบ</button></td></tr>';});
  container.innerHTML=html+'</tbody></table></div>';
}




function saveAdminRow(name,row,count){var vals=[];for(var i=0;i<count;i++)vals.push(document.getElementById('adm_'+row+'_'+i).value);google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r.success){Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',r.message,'success');loadAdminData();}else showError(r.message);}).apiAdminSaveRow(name,row,vals,currentUser);}
function deleteAdminRow(name,row){Swal.fire({title:'ยืนยันลบข้อมูลแถวนี้?',icon:'warning',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'ลบ',cancelButtonText:'ยกเลิก'}).then(function(x){if(!x.isConfirmed)return;google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){if(r.success){Swal.fire('สำเร็จ',r.message,'success');loadAdminData();}else showError(r.message);}).apiAdminDeleteRow(name,row,currentUser);});}
</script></body></html>`;
}
